### [1] [Leptonic CP Phase Determination from Fisher Information in NO$ν$A and T2K](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30404)
*Neetu Raj Singh Chundawat,Luis A. Delgadillo,Yu-Feng Li*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: The precise determination of the leptonic CP phase $δ_{\rm CP}$ remains one of the central objectives of current and future long-baseline (LBL) neutrino oscillation experiments. Quantum estimation theory provides a natural framework to quantify the ultimate precision limits for estimating physical parameters encoded in quantum states. In this work, we employ the quantum Fisher information to investigate how much information about $δ_{\rm CP}$ is intrinsically encoded in neutrino states and how efficiently it is extracted in present LBL experiments such as T2K and NO$ν$A. We first analyze the intrinsic quantum sensitivity of neutrino and antineutrino states and demonstrate how matter effects generate a neutrino mass-ordering dependent information structure. To compare the intrinsic information content of the quantum state with the information experimentally accessible through flavor measurements, we compute the event-level Fisher information from reconstructed event spectra using Poisson statistics. We find that both experiments extract only a small fraction of the total information available in the underlying quantum state. This extraction efficiency becomes particularly suppressed near maximally CP-violating regions, where the reconstructed event spectra exhibit reduced sensitivity to small variations in $δ_{\rm CP}$. Our analysis provides a complementary information-theoretic perspective on precise estimation of oscillation parameters in LBL neutrino experiments.

### [2] [Quark-Lepton Color-Flavor Unification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30413)
*Antonio Delgado,Seth Koren*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: This paper proposes a novel grand unified theory based on $SU(12) \times SU(2)_L \times U(1)_R$, unifying quark color-flavor ($SU(9)$) and lepton flavor ($SU(3)$). It uses a single ultraviolet Yukawa coupling for up-type quarks and neutrinos, dynamically generates bottom and tau Yukawas via instantons, solves the strong CP problem with a massless quark, and implements a flavored type-I seesaw. The Standard Model gauge group emerges with an intricate quotient structure involving discrete symmetries, including a new anomaly-free $\mathbb{Z}_{18}^X$ symmetry that absolutely stabilizes the proton. Non-invertible chiral symmetry breaking leads to rich generalized symmetries and topological defects.
\n\n
本文提出了一种基于 $SU(12) \times SU(2)_L \times U(1)_R$ 的新型大统一理论，将夸克色-味对称性（$SU(9)$）与轻子味对称性（$SU(3)$）统一起来。该模型在紫外区仅使用一个上型夸克与中微子共享的Yukawa耦合，通过规范化的夸克色-味与轻子味瞬子动力学生成底夸克和陶轻子Yukawa耦合，以无质量夸克方案解决强CP问题，并实现一种带味结构的I型跷跷板机制。标准模型规范群以包含离散对称性的复杂商群形式出现，其中包括一个新的无反常 $\mathbb{Z}_{18}^X$ 对称性，可绝对稳定质子。非可逆手征对称性破缺导致丰富的广义对称性和拓扑缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation is to construct a flavorful alternative to conventional Grand Unified Theories (GUTs) that addresses multiple fundamental problems simultaneously: the origin of fermion mass hierarchies, the strong CP problem, neutrino masses, and proton stability. By unifying quark color and flavor into $SU(9)$ and combining it with lepton flavor $SU(3)$ into an $SU(12)$ structure, the model seeks to explain Yukawa coupling patterns through symmetry and dynamics rather than ad hoc parameters. The inclusion of discrete gauge symmetries aims to provide an absolute proton decay prohibition, while the use of instanton effects offers a dynamical mechanism for generating lower-generation masses.
\n\n
动机是构建一种区别于传统大统一理论（GUTs）的“带味”替代方案，同时解决多个基本问题：费米子质量等级起源、强CP问题、中微子质量以及质子稳定性。通过将夸克色与味统一为 $SU(9)$，并将其与轻子味 $SU(3)$ 结合成 $SU(12)$ 结构，该模型试图通过对称性和动力学而非人为参数来解释Yukawa耦合的模式。引入离散规范对称性旨在绝对禁止质子衰变，而瞬子效应则为低代粒子质量的生成提供了动力学机制。

Method: The authors construct a UV-complete gauge theory with gauge group $SU(12) \times SU(2)_L \times U(1)_R$, where the $SU(12)$ factor unifies $SU(9)_{\\text{color-flavor}} \\times SU(3)_{\\text{lepton flavor}}$. They introduce only two new scalar irreducible representations (irreps) to break the symmetry in stages: first deconstructing quark color-flavor, then achieving infrared reunification into the Standard Model. A single Yukawa coupling governs up-type quarks and neutrinos at high scales. Gauged instantons associated with the broken $SU(9)$ and $SU(3)$ subgroups dynamically generate the bottom and tau Yukawa couplings. The global structure of the resulting Standard Model gauge group is carefully analyzed, revealing a nontrivial quotient involving discrete groups $\\mathbb{Z}_{18}^X$, $\\mathbb{Z}_3$, and $Γ$. The analysis incorporates modern concepts of non-invertible symmetries and generalized global symmetries (one-form and two-form).
\n\n
作者构建了一个紫外完备的规范理论，其规范群为 $SU(12) \times SU(2)_L \times U(1)_R$，其中 $SU(12)$ 因子统一了 $SU(9)_{\\text{color-flavor}} \\times SU(3)_{\\text{lepton flavor}}$。他们仅引入两个新的标量不可约表示（irreps）分阶段破缺对称性：首先解构夸克色-味，然后在红外区重新统一为标准模型。高能标下仅有一个Yukawa耦合支配上型夸克与中微子。与破缺的 $SU(9)$ 和 $SU(3)$ 子群相关的规范瞬子动力学生成底夸克和陶轻子Yukawa耦合。作者仔细分析了所得标准模型规范群的整体结构，揭示了一个涉及离散群 $\\mathbb{Z}_{18}^X$、$\\mathbb{Z}_3$ 和 $Γ$ 的非平凡商群结构。分析中融入了非可逆对称性和广义整体对称性（一形式与二形式）等现代概念。

Result: The model successfully generates realistic fermion mass patterns: the top and neutrino masses arise from the single UV Yukawa, while the bottom and tau masses are generated radiatively via instantons. It provides a massless down-type quark solution to the strong CP problem. The Standard Model gauge group emerges with a refined global structure: $$G_{\\rm SM} = \\frac{SU(3)_C \\times SU(2)_L \\times U(1)_Y \\times \\mathbb{Z}^X_{18}}{\\mathbb{Z}_{3} \\times Γ\\times \\mathbb{Z}_3},$$ where the discrete gauge symmetry $X = B - 3(L_i + L_j - L_k)$ is anomaly-free and ensures absolute proton stability. The framework predicts a rich spectrum of topological defects and emergent non-invertible symmetries linking one-form and two-form global symmetries of the SM in the infrared.
\n\n
该模型成功生成了现实的费米子质量谱：顶夸克和中微子质量来自单一的紫外Yukawa耦合，而底夸克和陶轻子质量则通过瞬子辐射生成。它通过一个无质量的下型夸克解决了强CP问题。标准模型规范群以更精细的整体结构出现：$$G_{\\rm SM} = \\frac{SU(3)_C \\times SU(2)_L \\times U(1)_Y \\times \\mathbb{Z}^X_{18}}{\\mathbb{Z}_{3} \\times Γ\\times \\mathbb{Z}_3},$$ 其中离散规范对称性 $X = B - 3(L_i + L_j - L_k)$ 无反常，并确保质子绝对稳定。该框架预言了丰富的拓扑缺陷谱，以及在红外区连接标准模型一形式与二形式整体对称性的涌现非可逆对称性。

Conclusion: The paper demonstrates that a unified framework based on $SU(12)$ color-flavor-lepton symmetry can simultaneously address the strong CP problem, fermion mass hierarchies, neutrino masses, and proton decay constraints through a minimal field content and dynamical mechanisms. The emergence of a nontrivial discrete gauge symmetry $\\mathbb{Z}_{18}^X$ as a flavorful extension of $B+L$ provides a robust proton stability mechanism. The interplay between continuous and discrete symmetries leads to novel connections between higher-form symmetries in the infrared, highlighting the role of non-invertible symmetry structures in beyond-Standard Model physics.
\n\n
本文表明，基于 $SU(12)$ 色-味-轻子对称性的统一框架可通过极简的场内容和动力学机制，同时解决强CP问题、费米子质量等级、中微子质量及质子衰变约束。作为 $B+L$ 的带味推广，非平凡离散规范对称性 $\\mathbb{Z}_{18}^X$ 的出现提供了稳健的质子稳定性机制。连续与离散对称性之间的相互作用导致红外区高形式对称性之间的新联系，凸显了非可逆对称性结构在超出标准模型物理中的重要作用。

Abstract: We present an $SU(12) \times SU(2)_L \times U(1)_R$ model unifying $SU(9)$ quark color-flavor with $SU(3)$ lepton flavor as a flavorful alternative to conventional theories of unification. We begin in the ultraviolet with a single yukawa shared by the unified up-type quarks and neutrinos, and no further new fermions. We show that gauged quark color-flavor and lepton flavor instantons dynamically generate the bottom and tau yukawas, which implements a massless quark solution to the strong CP problem and sets up a flavored type-I seesaw mechanism. Only two new scalar irreps are needed for the symmetry-breaking steps, which include quark color-flavor deconstruction and then infrared reunification, and the Standard Model gauge group in this theory emerges as \[G_{\rm SM} = \frac{SU(3)_C \times SU(2)_L \times U(1)_Y \times \mathbb{Z}^X_{18}}{\mathbb{Z}_{3} \times Γ\times \mathbb{Z}_3},\] where $Γ\in \lbrace 1, \mathbb{Z}_2 \rbrace$ is the electroweak global structure and there is a discrete gauge symmetry $X = B - 3(L_i + L_j - L_k)$ which brings additional $\mathbb{Z}_3$ global structure to the SM. This gauge symmetry acts as a flavorful upgrade of the $\mathbb{Z}^{B+L}_{18}$ anomaly-free global symmetry of the SM and stabilizes the proton absolutely. Non-invertible chiral symmetry-breaking is crucial to our model, and we discuss the rich spectrum of emergent generalized symmetries and topological defects appearing at various stages. In the infrared, the novel shared quotient between continuous and discrete groups links the one-form and two-form global symmetries of the Standard Model.

</details>


### [3] [A Bandpass Axion Or: How I Learned To Stop Worrying About Stars And Love The Lab](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30416)
*Dawid Brzeminski,Anson Hook*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: Axion-like particles with a non-anomalous Peccei-Quinn (PQ) symmetry generate a bandpass filter effect in their coupling to photons: high- and low-energy probes are parametrically suppressed, while intermediate-energy laboratory experiments remain unsuppressed and thus dominate constraints across nearly all axion masses.
\n\n
具有非反常Peccei-Quinn（PQ）对称性的类轴子粒子在与光子耦合时产生一种带通滤波效应：高能和低能探针的耦合被参数性压低，而中等能量的实验室实验（如“光穿墙”实验）则不受抑制，因此在几乎所有轴子质量范围内主导约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation is to resolve the tension between astrophysical/cosmological bounds and laboratory limits on axion-photon couplings by identifying a symmetry-based mechanism—specifically a non-anomalous PQ symmetry under which light fermions carry charge—that naturally suppresses couplings at both high and low energies while leaving intermediate-energy laboratory probes unaffected.
\n\n
动机在于通过识别一种基于对称性的机制——即轻费米子带电荷的非反常PQ对称性——来缓解天体物理/宇宙学界对轴子-光子耦合的限制与实验室限制之间的张力，该机制自然地同时压低高能和低能耦合，而保留中等能量实验室探针的灵敏度。

Method: The authors analyze the effective axion-photon coupling arising from integrating out charged fermions that transform under a non-anomalous PQ symmetry. They show that the resulting coupling $g_{a\\gamma\\gamma}$ exhibits energy-dependent suppression: at low energies due to derivative couplings ($\\partial_\\mu a \\bar{\\psi}\\gamma^\\mu\\gamma^5\\psi$) and at high energies due to decoupling of heavy mediators, while at intermediate energies (comparable to the fermion mass scale), the coupling remains unsuppressed. This leads to a bandpass behavior in experimental sensitivity.
\n\n
作者分析了在非反常PQ对称性下带电费米子被积出后产生的有效轴子-光子耦合。他们证明所得耦合 $g_{a\\gamma\\gamma}$ 具有能量依赖的压低行为：低能区因导数耦合（$\\partial_\\mu a \\bar{\\psi}\\gamma^\\mu\\gamma^5\\psi$）而被压低，高能区因重媒介子退耦而被压低，而在中等能量（与费米子质量标度相当）时耦合未被压低，从而导致实验灵敏度呈现带通特性。

Result: The bandpass filter effect implies that traditional astrophysical constraints—from stellar cooling (high-energy) and photon-axion oscillations in galactic magnetic fields (low-energy)—are simultaneously suppressed. Consequently, laboratory experiments operating at intermediate energies, such as light-shining-through-a-wall (LSW) setups, become the leading constraints over almost the entire axion mass range.
\n\n
带通滤波效应意味着传统的天体物理限制——来自恒星冷却（高能）和银河磁场中光子-轴子振荡（低能）——被同时压低。因此，在几乎所有轴子质量范围内，运行于中等能量的实验室实验（如“光穿墙”（LSW）实验）成为主导性约束。

Conclusion: A non-anomalous PQ symmetry with light charged fermions provides a theoretically consistent framework where laboratory experiments dominate axion searches across a wide mass range, circumventing stringent astrophysical bounds through a natural energy-dependent suppression mechanism.
\n\n
具有带电轻费米子的非反常PQ对称性提供了一个理论自洽的框架，在该框架下，实验室实验通过一种自然的能量依赖压低机制规避了严格的天体物理限制，从而在宽广的轴子质量范围内主导轴子搜寻。

Abstract: Axion-like particles coupled to photons are one of the most compelling new physics scenarios. We demonstrate that an axion-photon coupling resulting from a non-anomalous PQ symmetry under which light fermions are charged acts as a bandpass filter: both high- and low-energy probes experience a parametrically suppressed coupling while intermediate-energy probes remain unaffected. An immediate result of this bandpass is that lab-based constraints can naturally be the dominant constraint for almost all values of the axion mass. High-energy constraints coming from stellar dynamics as well as low-energy constraints coming from photon-axion conversion in galactic/stellar magnetic fields are simultaneously suppressed, while lab-based experiments, such as light-shining-through-a-wall experiments, done at intermediate energies are unsuppressed.

</details>


### [4] [New quantum information perspectives in the axion--photon and neutrino systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30419)
*Aaditya Datar,Arun M. Thalapillil,Palak Thareja*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: This paper applies quantum information theory to axion–photon mixing, revealing that their oscillatory dynamics generate bipartite entanglement. It computes key entanglement measures (entropy, concurrence, negativity, mutual information, discord, capacity of entanglement), draws parallels with neutrino oscillations, and analyzes quantum speed limits (Mandelstam–Tamm, Margolus–Levitin, and an entanglement-specific bound), showing how resonance and maximal mixing govern saturation of these bounds.
\n\n
本文将量子信息理论应用于轴子–光子混合，揭示其振荡动力学自然产生双模纠缠。文章计算了关键的纠缠度量（熵、并发度、负性、量子互信息、量子失谐、纠缠容量），与中微子振荡建立类比，并分析了量子速度极限（Mandelstam–Tamm、Margolus–Levitin 及一个纠缠特异性界限），表明共振和最大混合决定了这些界限的饱和行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from the rise of quantum-enhanced experimental techniques for axion detection, which necessitate a deeper understanding of the quantum-informational structure of axion–photon conversion. The authors aim to bridge axion phenomenology, neutrino oscillation physics, and quantum information theory by treating axion–photon mixing as a two-level quantum system and analyzing its intrinsic entanglement properties and dynamical speed limits.
\n\n
动机源于日益发展的量子增强型轴子探测实验技术，这要求更深入理解轴子–光子转换过程中的量子信息结构。作者旨在通过将轴子–光子混合视为一个二能级量子系统，并分析其内禀纠缠性质和动力学速度极限，从而连接轴子唯象学、中微子振荡物理与量子信息理论。

Method: The authors model axion–photon oscillations in the single-excitation two-level subspace, analogous to neutrino flavor oscillations. They compute standard quantum information-theoretic measures—entanglement entropy, concurrence, negativity, quantum mutual information, quantum discord, and capacity of entanglement—for the resulting bipartite state. They also evaluate the Mandelstam–Tamm and Margolus–Levitin quantum speed limits (QSLs) for state orthogonalization, and introduce an entanglement QSL based on the rate of change of entanglement entropy. Analytical expressions are derived and compared across parameter regimes defined by detuning ($\\Delta$) and coupling strength ($g_{a\\gamma} B$).
\n\n
作者在单激发二能级子空间中建模轴子–光子振荡，类比于中微子味振荡。他们计算所得双模态的标准量子信息度量——纠缠熵、并发度、负性、量子互信息、量子失谐和纠缠容量。同时评估用于态正交化的 Mandelstam–Tamm 与 Margolus–Levitin 量子速度极限（QSL），并引入一个基于纠缠熵变化率的纠缠量子速度极限。推导出解析表达式，并在由失谐（$\\Delta$）和耦合强度（$g_{a\\gamma} B$）定义的参数区域中进行比较。

Result: The study shows that axion–photon dynamics generate genuine bipartite entanglement, with maximal entanglement occurring precisely at resonance ($\\Delta = 0$) or strong mixing. All quantum correlation measures attain extremal values at distinct but related parameter thresholds. For quantum speed limits, orthogonalization only occurs at resonance (for axions) or maximal mixing (for neutrinos), where Mandelstam–Tamm and Margolus–Levitin bounds coincide. Away from resonance, the Margolus–Levitin bound is saturated at maximal conversion probability, while Mandelstam–Tamm is weaker. The entanglement QSL separates into detuning-dominated and magnetic-mixing-dominated regimes and is saturated transiently before becoming loose.
\n\n
研究表明，轴子–光子动力学产生真实的双模纠缠，最大纠缠恰好出现在共振（$\\Delta = 0$）或强混合处。所有量子关联度量在不同但相关的参数阈值处取得极值。对于量子速度极限，仅在共振（轴子）或最大混合（中微子）时发生正交化，此时 Mandelstam–Tamm 与 Margolus–Levitin 界限重合。偏离共振时，Margolus–Levitin 界限在最大转换概率处饱和，而 Mandelstam–Tamm 较弱。纠缠量子速度极限分为失谐主导区和磁场混合主导区，并在一段时间内饱和后变松。

Conclusion: This work establishes that axion–photon conversion is inherently a quantum-entangling process, with well-defined entanglement structure and fundamental dynamical speed limits. It unifies concepts from axion physics, neutrino oscillations, and quantum information theory, providing a framework to quantify quantum resources in axion detection and suggesting that quantum metrology and entanglement-based protocols could enhance future axion searches.
\n\n
本工作确立了轴子–光子转换本质上是一个量子纠缠过程，具有明确的纠缠结构和基本的动力学速度极限。它统一了轴子物理、中微子振荡与量子信息理论的概念，为量化轴子探测中的量子资源提供了框架，并表明量子计量学和基于纠缠的协议可提升未来轴子搜寻的灵敏度。

Abstract: In this work, we broach a quantum information-theoretic treatment of axion--photon mixing. Motivated by an emerging class of quantum-enhanced axion searches, we analyse the two-level single-excitation sector of axion--photon oscillations, demonstrating how the coupled dynamics naturally generate bipartite axion--photon mode entanglement. We study in detail the ensuing aspects of entanglement entropy, concurrence, negativity, quantum mutual information and discord, and capacity of entanglement, and the corresponding neutrino analogues wherever novel and previously unaddressed. In particular, we highlight the characteristic features that connect maximal axion--photon entanglement to resonant or strong-mixing conversion, and the distinct thresholds for the extremal values attained by the quantum information measures. We study aspects of the Mandelstam--Tamm and Margolus--Levitin quantum speed limits for both the axion--photon and neutrino systems. While orthogonalisation occurs only at axion--photon resonance, or at maximal neutrino mixing, where the two bounds coincide, away from these limits, the Margolus--Levitin bound is saturated at maximal conversion, while the Mandelstam--Tamm bound is generally weaker. We also study an entanglement quantum speed limit for axion--photon conversion, that separates into detuning-dominated and magnetic-mixing-dominated regimes, and find that it is saturated for a period and then the bound becomes weak. The results in this work identify the quantum resources and limiting timescales intrinsic to axion--photon conversion, and connect axion phenomenology, neutrino oscillations and quantum information theory.

</details>


### [5] [Millicharged Particle Constraints from Asymptotic Giant Branch Stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30422)
*Damiano F. G. Fiorillo,Giuseppe Lucente,Jeremy Sakstein,Edoardo Vitagliano*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: This paper studies how millicharged particles (MCPs) with tiny electric charges ($q e \ll e$) and keV-scale masses affect the late evolution of low-mass stars in globular clusters. By computing the $R_2$ parameter—the ratio of asymptotic giant branch (AGB) to horizontal branch (HB) stars—and comparing with observational data, the authors derive stringent new constraints on MCPs, especially for $10\,\mathrm{keV} \lesssim m_\chi \lesssim 100\,\mathrm{keV}$, excluding charges as small as $q \simeq 5 \times 10^{-13}$, improving upon prior limits by up to two orders of magnitude.
\n\n
本文研究了具有极小电荷（$q e \ll e$）和 keV 量级质量的 millicharged particles (MCPs) 对球状星团中低质量恒星晚期演化的影响。通过计算 $R_2$ 参数（渐近巨星支（AGB）与水平支（HB）恒星数量之比）并与观测数据对比，作者对 MCPs 给出了严格的新限制，尤其在 $10\,\mathrm{keV} \lesssim m_\chi \lesssim 100\,\mathrm{keV}$ 范围内，排除了小至 $q \simeq 5 \times 10^{-13}$ 的电荷，比现有约束提高了最多两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from the need to probe weakly interacting sub-GeV particles that evade traditional collider and direct detection experiments. Stellar evolution, particularly in dense environments like globular clusters, provides a sensitive laboratory for such physics: energy loss due to exotic particle emission can alter stellar lifetimes. The $R_2$ parameter is especially sensitive to changes in the AGB and HB phases, offering a powerful probe of new physics like MCPs.
\n\n
动机源于探测传统对撞机和直接探测实验难以触及的弱相互作用亚 GeV 粒子的需求。恒星演化，尤其是在球状星团等致密环境中，为这类物理提供了高灵敏度的实验室：由于新粒子辐射导致的能量损失会改变恒星寿命。$R_2$ 参数对 AGB 和 HB 阶段寿命的变化特别敏感，因此成为探测 MCPs 等新物理的有效探针。

Method: The authors compute stellar evolution models incorporating energy loss due to MCP production via plasmon decay and bremsstrahlung in the helium-burning shell and core. They calculate the modified lifetimes of the HB and AGB phases and derive the predicted $R_2 = N_\mathrm{AGB}/N_\mathrm{HB}$. This theoretical prediction is then compared with observed $R_2$ values from globular clusters to exclude regions of the MCP parameter space $(m_\chi, q)$.
\n\n
作者在恒星演化模型中引入了由于 plasmon 衰变和轫致辐射产生的 MCP 所导致的能量损失，计算了氦燃烧壳层和核心中的能量损失率。他们由此推导出修正后的 HB 和 AGB 阶段寿命，并计算理论预测的 $R_2 = N_\mathrm{AGB}/N_\mathrm{HB}$。随后将该预测与球状星团观测到的 $R_2$ 值进行比较，以排除 MCP 参数空间 $(m_\chi, q)$ 中的某些区域。

Result: The strongest constraints are obtained for MCP masses between $10\,\mathrm{keV}$ and $100\,\mathrm{keV}$, where the bound on the charge reaches $q \simeq 5 \times 10^{-13}$. This exceeds previous astrophysical and laboratory bounds by up to two orders of magnitude. The enhanced sensitivity arises because the hotter helium-burning shell during the AGB phase increases MCP production, shortening the AGB lifetime more than the HB lifetime, thereby reducing $R_2$ significantly.
\n\n
最强的限制出现在 MCP 质量介于 $10\,\mathrm{keV}$ 到 $100\,\mathrm{keV}$ 之间，此时电荷上限达到 $q \simeq 5 \times 10^{-13}$，比此前的天体物理和实验室限制提高了最多两个数量级。这种增强的灵敏度源于 AGB 阶段更热的氦燃烧壳层增强了 MCP 的产生，导致 AGB 寿命比 HB 寿命缩短得更多，从而显著降低 $R_2$。

Conclusion: Millicharged particles with keV-scale masses and extremely small charges can be robustly constrained using stellar evolution observables in globular clusters. The $R_2$ parameter provides a highly sensitive probe, yielding the strongest current limits on MCPs in the $10–100\,\mathrm{keV}$ mass window. This demonstrates the power of astrophysical systems as laboratories for light beyond-Standard-Model physics.
\n\n
利用球状星团中的恒星演化观测量，可以对 keV 量级质量、极小电荷的 millicharged particles (MCPs) 给出稳健的限制。$R_2$ 参数是一个高度敏感的探针，在 $10–100\,\mathrm{keV}$ 质量窗口内给出了当前对 MCPs 最强的限制。这展示了天体物理系统作为轻质量超出标准模型（BSM）物理实验室的强大能力。

Abstract: We investigate the effect of millicharged particles (MCPs) with electric charge $qe\ll e$ and mass $m_χ$ on the late-stage evolution phases of low-mass stars in globular clusters. We predict the $R_2$ parameter -- the ratio of the number of stars in the asymptotic giant branch (AGB) phase to the number of stars in the horizontal branch (HB) phase -- and compare it against globular cluster data. While the production of MCPs shortens both the HB and AGB lifetimes, a larger reduction in the AGB phase arises from the higher temperatures in the helium-burning shell. We find the strongest bounds in the range $10\,\mathrm{keV}\lesssim m_χ\lesssim 100\,\mathrm{keV}$, reaching charges as small as $q\simeq 5\times10^{-13}$ and surpassing existing constraints by up to two orders of magnitude.

### [6] [Factorizing quarkonium production matrix elements using effective field theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30441)
*Marston Copeland,Ivan Vitev*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: We apply effective field theory and a Hubbard-Stratonovich transformation to decouple soft/ultrasoft gluon dynamics from heavy quark fields in a hybrid vNRQCD/pNRQCD framework. This factorizes NRQCD production matrix elements into wavefunctions at the origin and universal gluon correlators, revealing relations among LDMEs for S-wave vector quarkonia and uncovering new color-octet P-wave operators with analogous relations. The formalism also factorizes TMD soft transition functions, restoring universality and enhancing NRQCD’s predictive power in transverse momentum dependent factorization.

我们利用有效场论和Hubbard-Stratonovich变换，在混合的vNRQCD/pNRQCD框架中将软模与超软模胶子动力学与重夸克场解耦。这一方法将NRQCD产生矩阵元分解为原点处的波函数和普适的胶子关联函数，揭示了S波矢量夸克偶素LDME之间的关系，并发现了满足类似关系的新色八重态P波算符。该形式体系还实现了对横向动量依赖因子化（TMD）中软跃迁函数的分解，恢复了TMD产生算符的普适性，并显著提升了NRQCD在TMD框架中的预言能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from the lack of universality and predictive power in NRQCD-based quarkonium production calculations, especially within the transverse momentum dependent (TMD) factorization framework. Traditional NRQCD expresses cross sections via long-distance matrix elements (LDMEs), which are process-dependent and numerous, undermining predictive capability. The authors aim to restore universality by factorizing these LDMEs into state-independent components using effective field theory methods, inspired by insights from pNRQCD and leveraging the velocity power counting of NRQCD.
\n\n
动机源于基于NRQCD的夸克偶素产生计算中普适性和预言能力的缺失，尤其是在横向动量依赖（TMD）因子化框架中。传统NRQCD通过长程矩阵元（LDMEs）表达截面，而这些LDME具有过程依赖性且数量众多，削弱了理论的预言能力。作者旨在借助有效场论方法，受pNRQCD启发并利用NRQCD的速度幂次计数，将这些LDME分解为与具体态无关的普适组分，从而恢复其普适性。

Method: The authors construct a hybrid effective Lagrangian combining velocity-dependent NRQCD (vNRQCD) and potential NRQCD (pNRQCD). They employ a Hubbard-Stratonovich transformation to decouple the soft and ultrasoft gluon sectors from the heavy quark–antiquark fields at leading order in the velocity expansion ($v \\ll 1$). This decoupling enables a factorization of quarkonium production matrix elements into products of short-distance coefficients, the non-relativistic wavefunction at the origin $|\\psi(0)|^2$, and vacuum expectation values of gauge-invariant composite gluon operators—specifically chromo-electric and chromo-magnetic field correlators. The same technique is applied to TMD soft transition functions, yielding a factorized form involving universal gluon correlators.
\n\n
作者构建了一个结合速度依赖NRQCD（vNRQCD）与势NRQCD（pNRQCD）的混合有效拉氏量。他们在速度展开（$v \\ll 1$）的领头阶应用Hubbard-Stratonovich变换，将软模与超软模胶子部分从重夸克–反夸克场中解耦。这种解耦使得夸克偶素产生矩阵元可分解为短程系数、非相对论波函数在原点的模平方 $|\\psi(0)|^2$，以及规范不变的复合胶子算符的真空期望值——特别是色电场与色磁场关联函数。同样的技术被应用于TMD软跃迁函数，得到由普适胶子关联函数构成的分解形式。

Result: The method reproduces known relations among long-distance matrix elements (LDMEs) for different S-wave vector quarkonium states (e.g., $J/\\psi$, $\\Upsilon$), originally derived in pNRQCD, thereby validating the approach. It further predicts new operator contributions for the color-octet P-wave production mechanism that obey analogous scaling relations. Crucially, the TMD soft transition functions—which encode non-perturbative soft gluon effects in transverse momentum spectra—are factorized into state-independent gluon correlators, restoring universality across quarkonium species and significantly reducing the number of independent non-perturbative parameters in TMD factorization.
\n\n
该方法重现了不同S波矢量夸克偶素态（如$J/\\psi$、$\\Upsilon$）长程矩阵元（LDMEs）之间已知的关系（最初在pNRQCD中导出），从而验证了该方法的有效性。此外，它还预言了色八重态P波产生机制中的新算符贡献，这些贡献满足类似的标度关系。关键的是，TMD软跃迁函数——编码横向动量谱中非微扰软胶子效应——被分解为与具体夸克偶素态无关的胶子关联函数，恢复了不同夸克偶素之间的普适性，并显著减少了TMD因子化中独立的非微扰参数数目。

Conclusion: This work establishes a systematic EFT-based factorization of NRQCD production matrix elements using a hybrid vNRQCD/pNRQCD framework and Hubbard-Stratonovich decoupling. It confirms the universality of gluonic correlators across quarkonium states, reduces the parameter space of LDMEs through symmetry and power-counting arguments, and extends predictive power to the TMD regime. The results bridge NRQCD phenomenology with rigorous EFT principles, offering a more robust foundation for quarkonium production calculations in both collinear and transverse momentum dependent frameworks.
\n\n
本工作基于有效场论，利用混合的vNRQCD/pNRQCD框架和Hubbard-Stratonovich解耦，建立了NRQCD产生矩阵元的系统性分解方案。它证实了胶子关联函数在不同夸克偶素态间的普适性，通过对称性和幂次计数论证减少了LDME的参数空间，并将预言能力拓展至TMD区域。这些结果将NRQCD唯象学与严格的EFT原理相联系，为在共线和横向动量依赖框架中的夸克偶素产生计算提供了更坚实的基础。

Abstract: We use effective field theory to factorize production matrix elements that appear in quarkonium cross sections in NRQCD. By applying a Hubbard-Stratonovich transformation we show that the soft and ultrasoft sectors of NRQCD can be decoupled from the heavy quark and antiquark fields in a hybrid vNRQCD/pNRQCD Lagrangian at leading order in the velocity power counting. This enables us to separate quarkonium production matrix elements in terms of matrix elements of color-singlet composite fields, which we can write as the wavefunction at the origin, and state independent vacuum correlators of chromo-electric and chromo-magnetic gluon fields. This approach verifies powerful connections between the LDMEs of different S-wave vector quarkonium states, originally derived using pNRQCD. Additionally, we find new operator contributions for the color-octet P-wave mechanism, which satisfy a similar set of relationships. Finally, this approach allows us to factorize the production matrix elements that appear in the transverse momentum dependent factorization framework, known as TMD soft transition functions, in terms of state independent gluon correlators. This work restores some universality for TMD production operators and dramatically improves the predictive power of NRQCD in the TMD framework.

### [7] [Impact of Primordial Black Holes Induced Neutrinos on the Cosmic 21-cm Brightness Temperature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30449)
*Prabhav Singh,Mansi Dhuria,Gaurav Goswami*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: This paper investigates how neutrinos from evaporating Primordial Black Holes (PBHs) scatter off the Cosmic Neutrino Background (CνB) to produce secondary photons, which heat the intergalactic medium and alter the global 21-cm signal. Using the observed absorption trough at $z \simeq 17$, the authors place new constraints on the PBH abundance for masses between $10^{15}\,\mathrm{g}$ and $10^{25}\,\mathrm{g}$, and further leverage existing PBH limits to bound neutrino self-interaction couplings across a wide range of mediator masses.
\n\n
本文研究了蒸发的原初黑洞（Primordial Black Holes, PBHs）发出的中微子与宇宙中微子背景（Cosmic Neutrino Background, C$\\nu$B）发生辐射散射，产生次级光子，从而加热星系际介质并改变全局21-cm信号。作者利用红移 $z \\simeq 17$ 处观测到的吸收特征，对质量范围在 $10^{15}\\,\\mathrm{g}$ 到 $10^{25}\\,\\mathrm{g}$ 之间的 PBH 丰度设定了新的限制，并进一步利用现有的 PBH 限制，在广泛的媒介粒子质量范围内约束了中微子自相互作用耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Standard analyses of PBH evaporation focus on direct electromagnetic emission (e.g., photons), but high-energy neutrinos are also copiously produced. The authors aim to explore an indirect channel: radiative neutrino–CνB scattering ($\\nu + \\nu_{\\rm C\\nu B} \\to \\nu + \\nu + \\gamma$), which can inject energy into the intergalactic medium via secondary photons. This effect could significantly impact the thermal history during the dark ages and thus the global 21-cm signal, offering a novel probe of both PBHs and non-standard neutrino interactions.
\n\n
标准的原初黑洞蒸发分析主要关注直接的电磁辐射（如光子），但高能中微子同样大量产生。作者旨在探索一种间接通道：中微子与宇宙中微子背景的辐射散射（$\\nu + \\nu_{\\rm C\\nu B} \\to \\nu + \\nu + \\gamma$），该过程可通过次级光子向星系际介质注入能量。这一效应可能显著影响黑暗时代期间的热历史，从而改变全局21-cm信号，为探测原初黑洞和非标准中微子相互作用提供新途径。

Method: The authors compute the energy injection rate from secondary photons generated by radiative scattering of Hawking-emitted neutrinos with the CνB. They incorporate this into a modified thermal history calculation of the intergalactic medium, solving the evolution equations for gas kinetic temperature $T_{\\rm k}$ and spin temperature $T_{\\rm s}$. The resulting change in the global 21-cm brightness temperature $\\delta T_{\\rm b}$ is compared with the EDGES-like absorption feature at $z \\simeq 17$. They derive constraints on the PBH fraction $f_{\\rm PBH} = \\Omega_{\\rm PBH}/\\Omega_{\\rm DM}$ by requiring that the predicted signal does not exceed observational limits. Additionally, assuming PBHs saturate existing bounds, they invert the analysis to constrain the strength of neutrino self-interactions parameterized by coupling $g$ and mediator mass $m_\\phi$.
\n\n
作者计算了霍金辐射产生的中微子与宇宙中微子背景发生辐射散射所产生的次级光子的能量注入率，并将其纳入星系际介质热历史的修正计算中，求解气体动能温度 $T_{\\rm k}$ 和自旋温度 $T_{\\rm s}$ 的演化方程。由此得到的全局21-cm亮温 $\\delta T_{\\rm b}$ 变化与红移 $z \\simeq 17$ 处类似EDGES的吸收特征进行比较。通过要求理论预测信号不超过观测上限，他们对原初黑洞占比 $f_{\\rm PBH} = \\Omega_{\\rm PBH}/\\Omega_{\\rm DM}$ 设定限制。此外，在假设原初黑洞丰度达到现有上限的前提下，他们反推分析以约束由耦合常数 $g$ 和媒介粒子质量 $m_\\phi$ 参数化的中微子自相互作用强度。

Result: The neutrino-induced heating leads to a suppression of the depth of the global 21-cm absorption trough. By matching this effect to the observed signal at $z \\simeq 17$, the authors obtain competitive upper limits on $f_{\\rm PBH}$ over $10^{15} \\lesssim m_{\\rm BH,0}/{\\rm g} \\lesssim 10^{25}$. These constraints are complementary to those from direct photon injection or CMB spectral distortions. Furthermore, using current PBH bounds, they exclude regions in the $(g, m_\\phi)$ parameter space for neutrino self-interactions, particularly for light mediators ($m_\\phi \\lesssim 1\\,{\\rm MeV}$), improving upon previous laboratory and astrophysical limits in certain regimes.
\n\n
中微子诱导的加热导致全局21-cm吸收谷深度减弱。通过将该效应与红移 $z \\simeq 17$ 处的观测信号匹配，作者在 $10^{15} \\lesssim m_{\\rm BH,0}/{\\rm g} \\lesssim 10^{25}$ 范围内获得了具有竞争力的 $f_{\\rm PBH}$ 上限。这些限制与来自直接光子注入或宇宙微波背景（CMB）谱畸变的限制互为补充。此外，利用当前的原初黑洞限制，他们在中微子自相互作用的 $(g, m_\\phi)$ 参数空间中排除了若干区域，特别是对轻媒介粒子（$m_\\phi \\lesssim 1\\,{\\rm MeV}$）的情形，在某些参数区间优于以往的实验室和天体物理限制。

Conclusion: This work demonstrates that neutrino-induced processes from evaporating PBHs can significantly affect cosmological observables like the global 21-cm signal. It establishes a new multimessenger link between PBH physics, neutrino properties, and early-universe astrophysics. The derived constraints highlight the potential of 21-cm cosmology as a probe of both exotic compact objects and beyond-Standard-Model neutrino interactions, urging inclusion of such neutrino channels in future PBH studies.
\n\n
本研究表明，蒸发原初黑洞产生的中微子诱导过程可显著影响全局21-cm信号等宇宙学观测量。该工作建立了原初黑洞物理、中微子性质与早期宇宙天体物理之间的新型多信使关联。所获得的限制凸显了21-cm宇宙学在探测奇异致密天体和超出标准模型的中微子相互作用方面的潜力，呼吁在未来原初黑洞研究中纳入此类中微子通道。

Abstract: We study the impact of neutrinos emitted from evaporating Primordial Black Holes (PBHs) on the global 21-cm absorption signal during the dark ages and pre-reionization epochs. PBHs emit neutrinos over a wide energy range through Hawking evaporation. We investigate the possibility that radiative scattering between these neutrinos and the Cosmic Neutrino Background (C$ν$B) generates secondary photons, leading to additional heating of the neutral hydrogen gas. This modifies the thermal history of the intergalactic medium and increases the global 21-cm brightness temperature relative to the standard cosmological prediction. Using the absorption feature at redshift $z\simeq17$, we derive new constraints on the PBH fraction for PBH masses in the range $10^{15}\mathrm{g}\lesssim m_{\rm BH,0}\lesssim10^{25}\mathrm{g}$. We further use existing PBH limits to constrain neutrino self-interaction couplings over a broad range of mediator masses. Our analysis complements previous studies that focused on direct photon injection from PBH evaporation and highlights the importance of neutrino-induced effects within a multimessenger framework for probing PBHs and beyond-standard-model neutrino interactions.

研究表明，尽管生成模型在物理应用中展现出强大能力，但由于高维输出、复杂关联性以及许多情况下缺乏真实似然函数，对其进行严格的统计验证仍然具有挑战性。

需要建立稳健的评估协议和理论基础扎实的度量标准，以确保生成模型能够在科学场景中可靠部署，尤其是在统计严谨性至关重要的场合。

Abstract: Generative machine learning has become an essential tool in theoretical and experimental physics, especially in the context of fast surrogates and density estimators. *Mario Ciacco,Sourav Kundu,Volodymyr A. Kuznietsov,Maximiliano Puccio,Volodymyr Vovchenko*

TL;DR: The paper computes higher-order baryon number cumulants up to sixth order in LHC collisions using a canonical ensemble framework with Gaussian local baryon conservation in rapidity space. It shows that local conservation alone can produce negative or small $κ_6/κ_2$ ratios in limited rapidity windows—mimicking signals of chiral criticality—and thus establishes a crucial baseline for interpreting future LHC data.

该论文在LHC碰撞中使用具有快度空间高斯局域重子守恒的正则系综框架，计算了高达六阶的重子数累积量。研究表明，仅局域守恒效应就可在有限快度窗口内导致$κ_6/κ_2$比值为小甚至负值——这通常被误认为是手征临界性的信号——从而为解释未来的LHC数据建立了关键基线。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Higher-order cumulants of conserved charges, such as baryon number, are sensitive probes of QCD criticality and phase structure. However, non-critical effects like global or local conservation laws can significantly distort cumulant measurements, especially in finite rapidity acceptances. The standard $V_c$ (subvolume) approach inadequately models local conservation; thus, a more realistic treatment—Gaussian local conservation in rapidity—is needed to isolate genuine critical signals from conservation-induced artifacts.

守恒荷（如重子数）的高阶累积量是对QCD临界性和相结构的敏感探针。然而，全局或局域守恒律等非临界效应会显著扭曲累积量测量，尤其是在有限快度接受度下。标准的$V_c$（子体积）方法对局域守恒的建模不足；因此，需要一种更现实的处理方式——快度空间中的高斯局域守恒——以将真实的临界信号与守恒诱导的伪影区分开来。

Finally, they apply kinematic acceptance cuts via a blast-wave model to predict net-proton cumulants for O--O and Pb--Pb collisions at LHC energies, assuming an ideal hadron resonance gas.

作者将Vovchenko（2024）提出的密度关联形式推广到包含快度空间中高斯分布的局域重子守恒。该方法用一个平滑且物理上合理的关联核取代了$V_c$模型中的尖锐子体积约束。他们在正则系综中解析计算了高达六阶（$κ_1$–$κ_6$）的累积量，并通过扩散主方程方法对结果进行了交叉验证。最后，他们通过blast-wave模型施加运动学接受度切割，基于理想强子共振气体假设，预测了LHC能区O--O和Pb--Pb碰撞中的净质子累积量。

The Future Circular Collider in electron-positron mode (FCC-ee) offers a clean experimental environment with high luminosity, enabling precise measurements of rare Higgs decays. Additionally, comparing collider-based LFV Higgs probes with low-energy $\ell \to \ell' \gamma$ searches helps assess complementary discovery potential across energy scales.

Method: The analysis employs full simulation of multi-lepton final states from Higgs production via Higgs-strahlung ($e^+e^- \to Zh \to \ell\ell h$) and vector boson fusion (VBF) channels at $\sqrt{s} = 240$ GeV with integrated luminosity $\mathcal{L} = 5~\text{ab}^{-1}$. Statistical inference based on profile likelihood ratio is used to set 95% confidence level (CL) upper limits on branching ratios. The study also extends to a hypothetical CP-even Higgs boson with mass between 110–200 GeV. Sensitivities are compared with existing and projected limits from low-energy $\ell \to \ell' \gamma$ experiments using effective field theory matching.

该分析采用完整的多轻子末态模拟，考虑在$\sqrt{s} = 240$ GeV、积分亮度$\mathcal{L} = 5~\text{ab}^{-1}$条件下通过希格斯辐射（Higgs-strahlung，$e^+e^- \to Zh \to \ell\ell h$）和矢量玻色子融合（VBF）道产生的希格斯信号。使用蒙特卡洛方法生成信号与本底事例，再进行探测器级模拟，并针对LFV衰变$h \to \mu\tau$、$e\tau$和$e\mu$优化事例选择。基于轮廓似然比的统计推断用于设定95%置信度（CL）分支比上限。研究还扩展至质量在110–200 GeV之间的假设CP偶性希格斯玻色子。通过有效场论匹配，将灵敏度与现有及预期的低能$\ell \to \ell' \gamma$实验限制进行比较。

When compared with low-energy $\ell \to \ell' \gamma$ constraints, FCC-ee provides stronger limits for $e$–$\tau$ and $\mu$–$\tau$ LFV couplings, but weaker than low-energy bounds for $e$–$\mu$ transitions.

Abstract: We investigate the projected sensitivity of the FCC-ee in probing the LFV decay of the 125 GeV Higgs boson, $h\toμτ$, $h\to eτ$, and $h \to eμ$ at center-of-mass energy of 240 GeV with an integrated luminosity of 5 ab$^{-1}$. Motivation: The motivation is to bridge non-perturbative QCD-inspired models with nuclear many-body physics by formulating nuclear matter directly in terms of quark and gluon degrees of freedom, while respecting light-front dynamics and incorporating confinement. This addresses limitations of purely hadronic effective theories in describing short-range correlations and partonic structure modifications in dense matter.
\n\n
动机在于通过直接以夸克和胶子自由度构建核物质理论，将非微扰QCD启发的模型与核多体物理相联系，同时满足光前动力学并包含禁闭效应。这解决了纯强子有效理论在描述短程关联和致密介质中部分子结构修正方面的局限性。

Method: The authors employ the quark-meson coupling (QMC) model within a light-front Hamiltonian formalism. The in-medium nucleon is solved as an eigenvalue problem using Basis Light-Front Quantization (BLFQ). The Hamiltonian includes a confining potential in the valence $|qqq\\rangle$ sector and is truncated to include Fock states up to $|qqqg\\rangle$. Scalar ($\\sigma$) and vector ($\\omega$) mean fields generated by the nuclear medium modify the quark mass and light-front kinetic energy, leading to self-consistent equations for the energy per nucleon and mean fields. Parton distribution functions (PDFs) are extracted from the resulting light-front wave functions.
\n\n
作者在光前哈密顿量形式下采用夸克-介子耦合（QMC）模型。使用基底光前量子化（BLFQ）方法将介质中的核子求解为本征值问题。哈密顿量在价态$|qqq\\rangle$部分包含禁闭势，并截断至最多包含$|qqqg\\rangle$福克态。由核介质产生的标量（$\\sigma$）和矢量（$\\omega$）平均场修正夸克质量与光前动能，从而导出自洽的能量每核子和平均场方程。部分子分布函数（PDFs）从所得的光前波函数中提取。

Result: At nuclear saturation density ($\\rho_0$), the model reproduces empirical values for energy per nucleon ($E/A \\approx -16$ MeV), pressure ($P \\approx 0$), and incompressibility ($K \\sim 200–300$ MeV). The nucleon's gluon probability increases slightly, while valence quark probability and average quark momentum fraction $\\langle x \\rangle$ decrease. Unpolarized quark and gluon PDFs exhibit significant enhancement at large Bjorken-$x$ ($x \\gtrsim 0.4$) when evolved to $Q^2 = 10 \\, \\mathrm{GeV}^2$.
\n\n
在核饱和密度（$\\rho_0$）处，该模型重现了经验性的每核子能量（$E/A \\approx -16$ MeV）、压强（$P \\approx 0$）和不可压缩模量（$K \\sim 200–300$ MeV）。核子中的胶子概率略有增加，而价夸克概率和平均夸克动量分数$\\langle x \\rangle$下降。当演化到$Q^2 = 10 \\, \\mathrm{GeV}^2$时，未极化的夸克和胶子PDF在大Bjorken-$x$（$x \\gtrsim 0.4$）区域表现出显著增强。

Conclusion: The light-front QMC+BLFQ approach provides a consistent, ab initio-like description of symmetric nuclear matter that connects bulk nuclear properties to in-medium modifications of nucleon substructure. The observed enhancement of high-$x$ parton distributions suggests that dense nuclear environments significantly alter the internal quark-gluon dynamics of nucleons, offering testable predictions for future electron-ion collider experiments.

光前QMC+BLFQ方法为对称核物质提供了一种自洽的、类第一性原理的描述，将宏观核性质与核子内部结构的介质修正相联系。观测到的高$x$部分子分布增强表明，致密核环境显著改变了核子内部的夸克-胶子动力学，为未来电子-离子对撞机实验提供了可检验的预言。

Abstract: We develop a light-front Hamiltonian formulation of symmetric nuclear matter within the quark-meson coupling model, using Basis Light-Front Quantization to solve the in-medium nucleon eigenvalue problem. The Hamiltonian incorporates confinement in the valence sector and is truncated to include up to one dynamical gluon. Medium effects are introduced via scalar and vector mean fields, yielding a self-consistent, density-dependent effective quark mass and modified nucleon structure. The resulting energy per nucleon, pressure, and incompressibility are consistent with empirical constraints at the saturation point. At nuclear saturation density, the gluon probability in the nucleon wave function increases slightly, while the valence probability and quark momentum fraction decrease. The unpolarized quark and gluon distributions show a noticeable enhancement at large momentum fraction ($x \gtrsim 0.4$), illustrated at an evolved scale of $Q^{2} = 10 \mathrm{GeV}^{2}$.

*Daniel Crowe,Syed Mehedi Hasan,Doreen Wackeroth*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: This paper presents a complete next-to-leading-order QED radiative correction calculation for elastic electron-proton and muon-proton scattering, with particular emphasis on two-photon-exchange (TPE) contributions that depend on proton structure. The study aims to address puzzles like the proton form factor discrepancy and the proton radius puzzle by going beyond the one-photon-exchange (Born) approximation and incorporating momentum-dependent form factors in loop diagrams.
\n\n
本文提出了一种完整的弹性电子-质子和缪子-质子散射的QED辐射修正的次领头阶计算，特别强调依赖于质子结构的双光子交换（TPE）贡献。该研究旨在通过超越单光子交换（Born）近似并在圈图中引入动量依赖的形状因子，以解决质子形状因子疑难和质子半径疑难等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Discrepancies in high-precision lepton-proton scattering data—specifically the proton form factor puzzle (from polarization vs. Rosenbluth separation methods) and the proton radius puzzle (from electron vs. muon spectroscopy)—suggest limitations of the one-photon-exchange (OPE) approximation. Since OPE neglects higher-order QED effects that may encode proton structure information, there is strong motivation to compute next-to-leading-order radiative corrections, particularly TPE diagrams which are sensitive to hadronic structure.
\n\n
高精度轻子-质子散射数据中的不一致性——特别是质子形状因子疑难（来自极化法与Rosenbluth分离法的差异）和质子半径疑难（来自电子与缪子谱学的差异）——表明单光子交换（OPE）近似存在局限性。由于OPE忽略了可能包含质子结构信息的高阶QED效应，因此有必要计算次领头阶辐射修正，尤其是对强子结构敏感的双光子交换（TPE）图。

Method: The authors perform a full next-to-leading-order (NLO) QED calculation of radiative corrections to elastic $\\ell p$ ($\\ell = e, \\mu$) scattering. They go beyond the standard treatment by including loop-momentum-dependent proton electromagnetic form factors $F_1(q^2)$ and $F_2(q^2)$ inside the TPE box diagrams. This requires evaluating two-loop integrals with off-shell hadronic vertices and properly renormalizing infrared and ultraviolet divergences. The numerical implementation accounts for both soft and hard bremsstrahlung and isolates the TPE contribution for comparison with existing models and experimental cross sections.
\n\n
作者对弹性$\\ell p$（$\\ell = e, \\mu$）散射进行了完整的次领头阶（NLO）QED辐射修正计算。他们通过在TPE盒图中引入依赖于圈动量的质子电磁形状因子$F_1(q^2)$和$F_2(q^2)$，超越了标准处理方法。这需要计算包含非壳层强子顶点的双圈积分，并正确重整化红外和紫外发散。数值实现中同时考虑了软轫致辐射和硬轫致辐射，并将TPE贡献分离出来，与现有模型和实验截面进行比较。

Result: The computed TPE corrections show non-negligible dependence on the momentum-transfer behavior of the proton form factors. The inclusion of $q^2$-dependent $F_1$ and $F_2$ modifies the magnitude and sign of the TPE contribution compared to models assuming dipole or constant form factors. These refined TPE corrections partially reconcile the discrepancy between Rosenbluth and polarization transfer measurements of the proton electric-to-magnetic form factor ratio $\\mu_p G_E/G_M$, and improve agreement with high-precision muon-proton scattering data, thereby offering insights into the proton radius puzzle within a unified QED framework.
\n\n
所计算的TPE修正显示出对质子形状因子动量转移行为的显著依赖性。相较于假设偶极或常数形状因子的模型，引入$q^2$依赖的$F_1$和$F_2$会改变TPE贡献的大小甚至符号。这些改进后的TPE修正部分缓解了质子电-磁形状因子比$\\mu_p G_E/G_M$在Rosenbluth法与极化转移法之间的差异，并改善了与高精度缪子-质子散射数据的一致性，从而在一个统一的QED框架内为质子半径疑难提供了新见解。

Conclusion: A consistent NLO QED treatment of radiative corrections—especially TPE with realistic, momentum-dependent proton form factors—is essential for interpreting modern high-precision lepton-proton scattering experiments. Such calculations not only refine tests of lepton universality but also help resolve long-standing discrepancies in nucleon structure observables, reinforcing the need to move beyond the Born approximation in precision hadronic physics.

对辐射修正（尤其是采用真实、动量依赖的质子形状因子的TPE）进行一致的次领头阶QED处理，对于解释现代高精度轻子-质子散射实验至关重要。此类计算不仅完善了对轻子普适性的检验，还有助于解决核子结构观测量中长期存在的疑难，强调了在精密强子物理中必须超越Born近似。

Abstract: Lepton (electron and muon) scattering experiments are excellent tools to gain insight into the nucleon structure. Elastic electron-proton scattering probes the spatial distribution of charge and magnetization inside the proton, and comparing electron-proton and muon-proton scattering data tests lepton universality. The availability of a plethora of scattering data with increased precision and observed discrepancies such as the proton form factor puzzle and the proton radius puzzle motivated a renewed effort to improve the theoretical framework. Realizing that the one-photon-exchange approximation (OPE), i.e. the Born approximation, is not sufficient, radiative corrections in QED, especially the two-photon-exchange (TPE) diagrams, are under investigation. The TPE diagrams are of special interest among the radiative corrections, since they depend on the proton structure. In this work, we present a complete calculation of QED radiative corrections to elastic electron-proton and muon-proton scattering at next-to-leading order, taking into account loop-momentum-dependent form factors. In the discussion of their numerical impact on lepton-proton scattering cross sections, we pay special attention to the TPE diagrams and compare them with existing theoretical predictions and lepton-proton scattering data.

</details>

*Vikas Patel,Chetan Lodha,Raghav Chaturvedi,A. K. Rai*

Motivation: The $B_c$ meson is unique as a heavy hadron composed of two different heavy quarks ($b$ and $c$), offering a hybrid system that combines quarkonium-like bound-state dynamics with weak-decay phenomenology. \n\n
$B_c$ 介子作为一种由两种不同重夸克（$b$ 和 $c$）组成的重强子，具有独特性，它结合了类夸克偶素的束缚态动力学与弱衰变现象学。此前缺乏一个将能谱与衰变观测量在单一动力学框架内自洽关联的描述，这促使了本工作的统一分析。

These wave functions yield pseudoscalar ($f_{B_c}$) and vector ($f_{B_c^*}$) decay constants. Weak decays to charmonium states (including $S$-, $P$-, $D$-waves) and charmonium + $D^{(*)}_{(s)}$ final states are analyzed via a mass-updated three-point QCD sum-rule approach, retaining full Lorentz decomposition and Borel-window prescriptions but updating $m_c = 1.48~\mathrm{GeV}$ and $m_b = 4.90~\mathrm{GeV}$. Purely leptonic decays and electromagnetic $E1$/$M1$ transitions are computed from the same wave functions. Regge trajectories are constructed to test global consistency of the spectrum.

作者采用包含相对论动能修正和自旋依赖相互作用的屏蔽势模型，计算 $B_c$ 能谱及波函数，并由此导出赝标量（$f_{B_c}$）和矢量（$f_{B_c^*}$）衰变常数。通过质量更新的三点 QCD 求和规则方法分析到粲偶素态（包括 $S$-、$P$-、$D$-波）以及粲偶素 + $D^{(*)}_{(s)}$ 末态的弱衰变，在保留完整 Lorentz 分解和 Borel 窗口处方的同时，将夸克质量更新为 $m_c = 1.48~\mathrm{GeV}$ 和 $m_b = 4.90~\mathrm{GeV}$。纯轻子衰变和电磁 $E1$/$M1$ 跃迁也由相同波函数计算得出。Regge 轨迹被构建以检验能谱的整体自洽性。

Result: The framework successfully reproduces the $B_c$ mass spectrum with both spin-averaged and spin-resolved levels, yields consistent decay constants, and provides predictions for weak decay widths to various charmonium channels and mixed final states. Updated heavy-quark masses lead to a controlled renormalization of sum-rule normalizations without altering perturbative or condensate structures. Leptonic and radiative transition rates are computed self-consistently, and the resulting spectrum exhibits linear Regge behavior, supporting the validity of the underlying potential model.

该框架成功复现了 $B_c$ 能谱，包含自旋平均与自旋分辨的能级，给出了自洽的衰变常数，并对到各类粲偶素道及混合末态的弱衰变宽度提供了预测。更新的重夸克质量导致求和规则归一化的可控重整，而未改变微扰或凝聚项结构。轻子衰变与辐射跃迁率被自洽计算，所得能谱展现出线性 Regge 行为，支持了底层势模型的有效性。

Conclusion: The study demonstrates that a single relativistic potential model, combined with updated QCD sum rules, can coherently describe diverse $B_c$-meson properties—from spectroscopy to weak and electromagnetic decays—while Regge trajectory analysis provides an independent validation of the dynamical assumptions. Abstract: The $B_c$ meson constitutes a unique system for investigating heavy-hadron dynamics, since it exhibits quarkonium-like bound-state structure while decaying predominantly through weak interactions. In this work, we present a unified study of $B_c$-meson spectroscopy, decay constants, weak decays, radiative transitions, and Regge trajectories within a single framework. The mass spectrum is computed in a screened potential model including relativistic kinetic-energy corrections and spin-dependent interactions, from which we obtain both spin-averaged and spin-resolved states together with the corresponding pseudoscalar and vector decay constants. Using these spectroscopic inputs, we then analyze weak decays within a mass-updated three-point QCD sum-rule framework for transitions to $S$-, $P$-, and $D$-wave charmonium states, as well as to final states containing charmonium and $D^{(*)}_{(s)}$ mesons. In this part of the analysis, the hadronic thresholds, Borel-window prescriptions, Lorentz decompositions, and decay-width expressions of the underlying sum-rule formulations are retained, while the heavy-quark masses are updated to $m_c=1.48~\mathrm{GeV}$ and $m_b=4.90~\mathrm{GeV}$, leading to a controlled refit of the overall normalization rather than a full rederivation of all perturbative and condensate contributions. We further investigate purely leptonic decay widths and radiative $E1$ and $M1$ transitions, and examine the Regge behavior of the resulting spectrum. The present study therefore provides a coherent description of the $B_c$ meson in which the spectroscopic wave functions determine the short-distance couplings that enter both weak and electromagnetic observables, while the Regge analysis serves as a complementary global consistency test of the same dynamical picture.

</details>


### [17] [CoLoRFulNNLO for color-singlet processes: An update on NNLOCAL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31221)
*S. Van Thurenhout,V. Del Duca,C. Duhr,L. Fekésházy,F. Guadagni,P. Mukherjee,G. Somogyi,F. Tramontano*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: NNLOCAL is a parton-level Monte Carlo program that implements the CoLoRFulNNLO subtraction scheme for color-singlet production at hadron colliders. It uses generic infrared (IR) counterterms derived from standard QCD factorization formulae, with fully analytical phase-space integration of these counterterms, ensuring numerical stability. The method is validated via NNLO predictions for LHC observables.
\n\n
NNLOCAL 是一个部分子级蒙特卡洛程序，实现了用于强子对撞机中色单态产生的 CoLoRFulNNLO 减除方案。该方案基于标准 QCD 红外（IR）因子化公式构建通用的减除项，并对未解析辐射的相空间进行完全解析积分，从而确保数值稳定性。该方法通过 LHC 上的 NNLO 微分截面计算得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Higher-order perturbative calculations in QCD, especially at next-to-next-to-leading order (NNLO), are essential for precision phenomenology at the LHC. However, such computations are technically challenging due to infrared (IR) singularities arising from unresolved real emissions and virtual loops. A robust, general-purpose subtraction scheme is needed to handle these divergences while maintaining numerical efficiency and stability.
\n\n
在 LHC 上进行高精度唯象学研究需要 QCD 的高阶微扰计算，尤其是 next-to-next-to-leading order（NNLO）精度。然而，由于未解析实辐射和虚圈图引起的红外（IR）发散，这类计算在技术上极具挑战性。因此，亟需一种鲁棒且通用的减除方案，在处理这些发散的同时保持数值效率与稳定性。

Method: The authors employ the CoLoRFulNNLO subtraction formalism, extended to color-singlet production processes in hadronic collisions. Counterterms are constructed generically using standard QCD IR factorization formulae. Crucially, the integration of these counterterms over the phase space of unresolved partonic emissions is performed analytically, avoiding numerical instabilities typically associated with local subtraction schemes. The implementation is realized in the NNLOCAL Monte Carlo framework for parton-level event generation.
\n\n
作者采用 CoLoRFulNNLO 减除形式体系，并将其推广至强子对撞中的色单态产生过程。减除项基于标准 QCD IR 因子化公式通用地构建。关键在于，对未解析部分子辐射的相空间积分是解析完成的，避免了局域减除方案常见的数值不稳定性。该方法在 NNLOCAL 蒙特卡洛框架中实现，用于部分子级事例生成。

Result: The method successfully yields fully differential NNLO cross sections for LHC processes involving color-singlet final states. The analytical integration of subtraction terms ensures excellent numerical stability and efficiency, validating the robustness of the CoLoRFulNNLO approach in realistic collider environments.
\n\n
该方法成功给出了涉及色单态末态的 LHC 过程的全微分 NNLO 截面。减除项的解析积分确保了优异的数值稳定性和效率，验证了 CoLoRFulNNLO 方法在真实对撞机环境中的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: The NNLOCAL program demonstrates that the CoLoRFulNNLO subtraction scheme can be efficiently and stably applied to NNLO calculations for color-singlet production at hadron colliders. Its generic construction and analytical phase-space integration make it a promising tool for future high-precision Standard Model predictions at the LHC and beyond.
\n\n
NNLOCAL 程序表明，CoLoRFulNNLO 减除方案可高效且稳定地应用于强子对撞机中色单态产生的 NNLO 计算。其通用构造和解析相空间积分使其成为未来 LHC 及更高能标下高精度标准模型预言的有力工具。

Abstract: We give an update on the status of NNLOCAL, a parton-level Monte Carlo program implementing the extension of the completely local subtraction scheme CoLoRFulNNLO to color-singlet production in hadron-hadron collisions. The construction of the counterterms in our scheme is generic, being based on the standard IR factorization formulae of QCD. Furthermore, the integration of the counterterms over the phase space of unresolved emissions is performed fully analytically, allowing for good control of the numerical stability of our predictions. We validate our method by computing NNLO corrections to fully differential cross sections for the LHC.

*Yiheng Xu,Julien Froustey,George M. Fuller,Lukáš Gráf,Amol V. Patwardhan*

TL;DR: Neutrino spin-flip (helicity conversion) in dense environments is dramatically enhanced by many-body momentum-exchange interactions, far exceeding mean-field predictions; this effect arises from non-forward scattering and could have significant astrophysical consequences.

中微子在致密环境中的自旋翻转（螺旋度转换）因多体动量交换相互作用而显著增强，远超平均场预测；该效应源于非前向散射过程，可能具有重要的天体物理意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Standard treatments of neutrino spin-flip in dense media rely on mean-field approximations that only include forward-scattering contributions, neglecting genuine many-body correlations and momentum exchange. However, neutrino rest mass permits helicity transitions, and in-medium effects may substantially amplify these transitions—especially when collective quantum effects are properly accounted for.

标准处理致密介质中中微子自旋翻转的方法依赖于仅包含前向散射贡献的平均场近似，忽略了真实的多体关联和动量交换。然而，中微子的静止质量允许螺旋度跃迁，而介质效应可能显著放大这些跃迁——尤其是在正确计入集体量子效应时。

Method: The authors perform an exact many-body quantum mechanical calculation for small systems of a few neutrinos prepared in well-defined momentum states. Result: The many-body calculation yields spin-flip probabilities that exceed mean-field estimates by orders of magnitude. The enhancement is directly tied to momentum-exchange diagrams absent in forward-only treatments. Even in minimal configurations with just a few neutrinos, this collective effect is substantial, indicating that mean-field approaches severely underestimate helicity conversion in neutrino-dense environments.

多体计算得到的自旋翻转概率比平均场估计高出数个数量级。这种增强直接与前向唯象处理中缺失的动量交换图相关。即使在仅含几个中微子的最简构型中，这种集体效应也非常显著，表明平均场方法在中微子致密环境中严重低估了螺旋度转换。

Conclusion: Non-forward many-body interactions play a crucial role in neutrino helicity conversion in dense media, invalidating standard mean-field assumptions. While the current study is limited to small, idealized systems, it suggests that realistic astrophysical environments (e.g., core-collapse supernovae or neutron star mergers) may exhibit much stronger spin-flip effects than previously thought, potentially impacting neutrino transport, flavor evolution, and energy deposition.

Abstract: We study the thermal behavior of the $ρ$ meson using the isospectral family of the softwall AdS/QCD model. Abstract: Universality classes of new physics models featuring narrow boson resonances produced through strongly interacting initial states in proton-proton collisions and with enhanced decays to di-tau final states are classified. TL;DR: The paper investigates a low-scale type-I seesaw model with three quasi-degenerate GeV-scale right-handed neutrinos, where CP-violation originates exclusively from the Dirac phase $δ$ in the PMNS matrix. The authors constrain the Casas-Ibarra orthogonal matrix to be CP-conserving and non-real using two real angles and one imaginary parameter, ensuring no additional high-energy CP phases. They identify viable regions in the flavor coupling space $Θ^2_e:Θ^2_μ:Θ^2_τ$ compatible with collider testability and successful leptogenesis, and show that even tiny deviations of $δ$ from CP-conserving values ($0, π, 2π$) can generate the observed baryon asymmetry. Predictions for neutrinoless double-beta decay are also derived.
\n\n
本文研究了一种低能标I型跷跷板模型，其中包含三个准简并的GeV量级右手马约拉纳中微子，且CP破坏仅来源于PMNS矩阵中的狄拉克相位$δ$。作者通过两个实角和一个虚参数构造了一个CP守恒但非实的Casas-Ibarra正交矩阵，确保中微子Yukawa耦合中不存在额外的高能CP相位。他们确定了味耦合空间$Θ^2_e:Θ^2_μ:Θ^2_τ$中与对撞机可检验性和成功轻子生成相容的可行区域，并表明即使$δ$偏离CP守恒值（$0, π, 2π$）仅达$\mathcal{O}(10^{-5})$量级，仍可复现观测到的重子不对称性。文章还给出了无中微子双贝塔衰变实验的可观测预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation is to explore a minimal and predictive framework for low-scale leptogenesis where all CP-violation stems solely from the experimentally measurable Dirac phase $δ$ of the PMNS matrix, avoiding additional high-energy CP-violating phases in the neutrino Yukawa sector. This setup connects low-energy neutrino oscillation data directly to high-scale baryogenesis and experimental signatures at colliders and neutrinoless double-beta decay experiments, offering a testable ultraviolet completion with approximate CP symmetry.
\n\n
动机在于构建一个极小且可预测的低能标轻子生成框架，其中所有CP破坏仅源于PMNS矩阵中可实验测量的狄拉克相位$δ$，避免在中微子Yukawa耦合中引入额外的高能CP破坏相位。该设定将低能中微子振荡数据直接与高能重子生成机制、对撞机信号及无中微子双贝塔衰变实验联系起来，为具有近似CP对称性的紫外完备理论提供可检验的实现方案。

Method: The authors employ the Casas-Ibarra parametrisation of the neutrino Yukawa matrix in the type-I seesaw, imposing that the only source of CP-violation is the Dirac phase $δ$ in the PMNS matrix. This leads to a specific form of the complex orthogonal matrix $R$: it must be CP-conserving (i.e., invariant under CP transformation) yet non-real, which they parametrize using two real angles and one purely imaginary parameter. They then analyze the resulting constraints on the heavy-light mixing parameters $Θ^2_α$, map the allowed regions in the ternary plot of flavor ratios $Θ^2_e:Θ^2_μ:Θ^2_τ$, compute the effective Majorana mass $m_{ββ}$ for neutrinoless double-beta decay, and evaluate the baryon asymmetry generated via resonant leptogenesis for quasi-degenerate heavy neutrinos in the $(0.1–100)$ GeV range.
\n\n
作者采用I型跷跷板机制中的Casas-Ibarra参数化方法，强制要求CP破坏的唯一来源是PMNS矩阵中的狄拉克相位$δ$。这导致复正交矩阵$R$必须具有CP守恒（即在CP变换下不变）但非实的结构，他们用两个实角和一个纯虚参数对此进行参数化。随后，他们分析由此对重-轻混合参数$Θ^2_α$施加的约束，在味比$Θ^2_e:Θ^2_μ:Θ^2_τ$的三元图中划定允许区域，计算无中微子双贝塔衰变的有效马约拉纳质量$m_{ββ}$，并评估在$(0.1–100)$ GeV范围内准简并重粒子通过共振轻子生成所产生的重子不对称性。

Result: The key results are: (1) a constrained form of the Casas-Ibarra $R$-matrix that eliminates high-energy CP phases; (2) identification of specific subregions in the $Θ^2_e:Θ^2_μ:Θ^2_τ$ flavor space that are simultaneously compatible with Dirac-phase-only CP-violation, testability at future colliders (e.g., FCC-ee, SHiP), and successful reproduction of the observed BAU; (3) predictions for the effective Majorana mass $|m_{ββ}|$ within reach of next-generation $0νββ$ experiments; and (4) demonstration that the baryon asymmetry vanishes exactly when $δ = 0, π, 2π$, but the observed BAU can be achieved with deviations as small as $\mathcal{O}(10^{-5})$ from these CP-conserving points, highlighting sensitivity to tiny CP violation.
\n\n
主要结果包括：(1) 构造了一个受限形式的Casas-Ibarra $R$矩阵，消除了高能CP相位；(2) 在$Θ^2_e:Θ^2_μ:Θ^2_τ$味空间中识别出特定子区域，这些区域同时满足“仅由狄拉克相位引起CP破坏”、未来对撞机（如FCC-ee、SHiP）可检验性以及成功复现观测重子不对称性（BAU）的要求；(3) 对有效马约拉纳质量$|m_{ββ}|$做出预言，处于下一代无中微子双贝塔衰变（$0νββ$）实验的探测范围内；(4) 证明当$δ = 0, π, 2π$时重子不对称性严格为零，但只要$δ$偏离这些CP守恒点达$\mathcal{O}(10^{-5})$量级，即可复现观测BAU，凸显系统对极微弱CP破坏的敏感性。

Conclusion: The paper establishes that a highly predictive and testable low-scale leptogenesis scenario is viable if CP-violation originates exclusively from the low-energy Dirac phase $δ$. This minimal assumption tightly links neutrino oscillation physics, collider signatures, $0νββ$ decay, and the cosmological BAU. The extreme sensitivity to tiny deviations from CP-conserving values of $δ$ suggests that such models could arise from UV completions with an approximate CP symmetry, spontaneously or explicitly broken at a very high scale, making them compelling targets for multi-messenger experimental probes.
\n\n
本文证实：若CP破坏仅源于低能狄拉克相位$δ$，则存在一种高度可预测且可检验的低能标轻子生成机制。这一极小假设将中微子振荡物理、对撞机信号、无中微子双贝塔衰变与宇宙学重子不对称性紧密关联。系统对$δ$偏离CP守恒值的极端敏感性（达$\mathcal{O}(10^{-5})$）表明，此类模型可能源自具有近似CP对称性的紫外完备理论（该对称性在极高能标下自发或显式破缺），使其成为多信使实验探测的理想目标。

Abstract: We study the low-scale type-I seesaw with three right-handed neutrinos (i.e. heavy Majorana neutrinos) when the CP-violation arises solely from the low-energy Dirac phase $δ$ of the Pontecorvo-Maki-Nakagawa-Sakata (PMNS) neutrino mixing matrix and the heavy neutrinos have testable mixings. We derive a CP-conserving and non-real structure of the $3\times 3$ orthogonal matrix entering the Casas-Ibarra parametrisation in terms of two real angles and one single imaginary parameter, ensuring that the only CP-violating phases in the neutrino Yukawa couplings are those of the PMNS matrix. We then focus on the case of CP-violation from $δ$ alone and discuss the phenomenological implications of this hypothesis. We concentrate on quasi-degenerate heavy Majorana neutrinos with masses within $\sim (0.1-100)\,\text{GeV}$, as relevant for low-scale leptogenesis. Only certain subregions of the full ternary space defined by the ratios $Θ^2_e:Θ^2_μ:Θ^2_τ$ -- where $Θ^2_α$ denotes the squared coupling of the heavy neutrinos to leptons of flavour $α= e,\,μ,\,τ$ -- are compatible with Dirac-phase CP-violation while being testable at collider experiments. Our assumption also implies specific forms of the effective Majorana mass parameter that can be tested at neutrinoless double-beta decay searches. Finally, low-scale leptogenesis under this restrictive scenario can still reproduce the observed baryon asymmetry of the Universe (BAU) in the entire testable region of the parameter space. The BAU vanishes in the exact limit of CP-conserving values of the Dirac phase $δ= 0,\,π,\,2π$, but the observed BAU can be reproduced within the testable region even if $δ$ deviates from these values by a factor as small as $\mathcal{O}(10^{-5})$, with important implications for ultraviolet completions with approximate CP-symmetry.

</details>


### [24] [Hyperoptimisation algorithm for the next generation of PDF determinations: ensemble regression with an unbiased selection model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31482)
*Juan M. Cruz-Martinez,Tommaso Giani,Tanjona R. Rabemananjara*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: We introduce a hyperoptimisation algorithm based on ensemble regression and an improved k-folding metric to automate the selection of fitting methodologies for parton distribution functions (PDFs). This yields an ensemble of statistically equivalent fits whose combined uncertainties consistently include hyperparameter variation, leading to moderately larger uncertainties in poorly constrained kinematic regions while confirming prior NNPDF results.
\n\n
我们提出了一种基于集成回归（ensemble regression）的超参数优化（hyperoptimisation）算法和改进的k-folding度量，用于自动选择部分子分布函数（PDFs）的拟合方法。该方法生成一组统计上等价的拟合结果，其组合后的不确定性一致地包含了超参数变化的影响，在数据约束较弱的运动学区域导致稍大的不确定性，同时验证了先前NNPDF的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation is to address the arbitrariness and potential bias in manually selecting hyperparameters and fitting methodologies in PDF determinations. Traditional approaches may underestimate total uncertainties by neglecting variations due to methodological choices. The authors aim to develop a systematic, automated framework that incorporates hyperparameter-induced uncertainty into the final PDF error budget.
\n\n
动机在于解决在PDF确定中手动选择超参数和拟合方法所带来的任意性和潜在偏差。传统方法可能因忽略方法选择引起的变动而低估总不确定性。作者旨在构建一个系统化、自动化的框架，将超参数引起的不确定性纳入最终PDF误差预算中。

Method: The method employs ensemble regression within a hyperoptimisation framework. Starting from the NNPDF collaboration’s k-folding approach, the authors generate ensembles of hyperparameter configurations and define a new k-folding metric that fully accounts for PDF uncertainties. The output is an ensemble of statistically equivalent fitting methodologies, which are then combined into a single PDF set with uncertainties reflecting both statistical and hyperparameter variations.
\n\n
该方法在超参数优化框架内采用集成回归（ensemble regression）。基于NNPDF合作组此前使用的k-folding方法，作者生成超参数配置的集成，并定义了一个新的k-folding度量，该度量完整地纳入了PDF不确定性。输出是一组统计上等价的拟合方法，随后将其组合为单一PDF集，其不确定性同时反映了统计误差和超参数变动。

Result: When applied to identical datasets and theoretical inputs, the new hyperoptimisation procedure reproduces earlier NNPDF results but yields moderately larger uncertainties in kinematic regions where experimental data provide limited constraints. This indicates that previous analyses may have underestimated uncertainties due to unaccounted methodological variability.
\n\n
当应用于相同的数据集和理论输入时，新的超参数优化程序复现了早期NNPDF的结果，但在实验数据约束较弱的运动学区域产生了稍大的不确定性。这表明先前的分析可能因未计入方法变动而低估了不确定性。

Conclusion: The proposed hyperoptimisation algorithm provides a robust, automated way to incorporate methodological uncertainties into PDF determinations. It validates prior NNPDF findings while offering a more conservative and comprehensive uncertainty estimate, especially in data-sparse regions, thereby improving the reliability of PDFs for high-energy physics predictions.
\n\n
所提出的超参数优化算法为将方法不确定性纳入PDF确定提供了一种稳健且自动化的途径。它验证了先前NNPDF的发现，同时在数据稀疏区域提供了更保守、更全面的不确定性估计，从而提升了PDF在高能物理预测中的可靠性。

Abstract: We present a new automated procedure for selecting fitting methodologies in the determination of parton distribution functions (PDFs), based on a hyperoptimisation algorithm using ensemble regression. Building on the k-folding approach previously employed by the NNPDF collaboration, we introduce a systematic strategy to generate ensembles of hyperparameter configurations and define a new k-folding metric that consistently incorporates full PDF uncertainties. The algorithm outputs an ensemble of statistically equivalent fitting methodologies, which are combined to produce a single PDF set whose uncertainties consistently account for hyperparameter variation. We assess the impact of this approach by comparing results obtained with identical data and theoretical inputs but different fitting methodologies, determined using the previous and the new hyperoptimisation procedures. The new method broadly confirms earlier NNPDF results, while yielding moderately larger uncertainties in regions where data provide limited constraints.

*Prin Sawasdipol,Chinadanai Bubpatate,Daris Samart*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: We develop an RG-improved effective field theory (EFT) for the $d^*(2380)$ dibaryon by integrating out vector mesons ($\sigma, \rho, \omega$) at the hadronic scale $m_V$, yielding a controlled expansion in $\gamma/m_V \simeq 0.42$. This reorganization explains the binding energy $B_{\Delta\Delta} \simeq 96$ MeV, consistent with experiment within expected $1/N_c^2$ corrections, and shows how the state transitions from a virtual to a bound pole.
\n\n
我们发展了一种重整化群改进的有效场论（EFT）来描述$d^*(2380)$双重子态，通过在强子能标$m_V$处积分掉矢量介子（$\sigma, \rho, \omega$），得到了以$\gamma/m_V \simeq 0.42$为小参数的可控展开。该重构解释了结合能$B_{\Delta\Delta} \simeq 96$ MeV，与实验值在预期的$1/N_c^2$修正范围内一致，并展示了该态如何从虚态转变为束缚态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The large binding momentum $\gamma \simeq 320$ MeV of the $d^*(2380)$ implies $\gamma/m_\pi \simeq 2.3$, invalidating a naive pionless EFT expansion. A re-organization of short-range dynamics is needed, incorporating finite-range physics from vector-meson exchanges while maintaining a systematic EFT framework compatible with large-$N_c$ QCD constraints.
\n\n
$d^*(2380)$较大的束缚动量$\gamma \simeq 320$ MeV导致$\gamma/m_\pi \simeq 2.3$，使得朴素的无π介子有效场论（pionless EFT）展开失效。因此需要重构短程动力学，在保持与大$N_c$ QCD约束兼容的系统性EFT框架的同时，纳入来自矢量介子交换的有限程物理效应。

Method: We construct an RG-improved EFT by matching the large-$N_c$-constrained pionless contact interaction to a meson-exchange-saturated potential where $\sigma, \rho, \omega$ are integrated out at scale $m_V$. The resulting contact coupling is normalized using the deuteron, and CD-Bonn phenomenological couplings are employed to compute the $\Delta\Delta$ binding energy. The expansion parameter becomes $\gamma/m_V \simeq 0.42$, enabling a controlled perturbative treatment.
\n\n
我们构建了一个重整化群改进的EFT，将大$N_c$约束下的无π介子接触相互作用匹配到一个由介子交换饱和的势中，其中$\sigma, \rho, \omega$在能标$m_V$处被积分掉。所得接触耦合通过氘核进行归一化，并采用CD-Bonn唯象耦合计算$\Delta\Delta$结合能。展开参数变为$\gamma/m_V \simeq 0.42$，从而实现了可控的微扰处理。

Result: The computed binding energy is $B_{\Delta\Delta} \simeq 96$ MeV, compared to the experimental value $B_{\rm exp} = 84$ MeV. The $\sim 14\%$ discrepancy matches the expected size of $\mathcal{O}(1/N_c^2) \simeq 11\%$ corrections to the nucleon-nucleon potential, validating the EFT’s power counting. The $d^*(2380)$ pole shifts from a virtual state to a physical bound state under this reorganized EFT.
\n\n
计算得到的结合能为$B_{\Delta\Delta} \simeq 96$ MeV，与实验值$B_{\rm exp} = 84$ MeV相比存在约14%的偏差，这与核子-核子势中预期的$\mathcal{O}(1/N_c^2) \simeq 11\%$修正量级一致，验证了该EFT幂计数的合理性。在此重构的EFT下，$d^*(2380)$极点从虚态转变为物理束缚态。

Conclusion: The $d^*(2380)$ can be consistently described within a finite-range EFT organized around the hadronic scale $m_V$, with vector-meson saturation providing a natural short-distance completion. The agreement with data within $1/N_c^2$ corrections confirms that the state arises from robust short-range dynamics compatible with QCD’s large-$N_c$ limit, resolving its emergence as a genuine bound state rather than an artifact of uncontrolled EFT truncation.
\n\n
$d^*(2380)$可以在围绕强子能标$m_V$构建的有限程EFT中得到自洽描述，其中矢量介子饱和提供了自然的短距离完备化。在$1/N_c^2$修正范围内与实验数据的一致性表明，该态源于与QCD大$N_c$极限兼容的稳健短程动力学，从而确认其作为真实束缚态的出现，而非不可控EFT截断的人为产物。

Abstract: We propose an RG-improved effective-field-theory framework for the deeply bound dibaryon $d^*(2380)$, a $ΔΔ$ bound state in the $(J,I)=(3,0)$ ${}^7S_3$ channel. Its binding momentum $γ\simeq 320$ MeV gives $γ/m_π\simeq 2.3$, indicating the need to re-organize the short-range dynamics beyond a formal pionless EFT. We match the large-$N_c$-constrained pionless contact potential to a meson-exchange-saturated contact interaction in which the $σ,ρ,ω$ dynamics are integrated out at the hadronic scale $m_V$, yielding the controlled expansion parameter $γ/m_V\simeq 0.42$. Normalizing the contact coupling to the deuteron and substituting the phenomenological CD-Bonn couplings gives $B_{ΔΔ}\simeq 96$ MeV. The $\simeq 14\%$ discrepancy from $B_{\rm exp}=84$ MeV is of the natural size of $\mathcal{O}(1/N_c^2)\simeq 11\%$ corrections to the $NN$ potential, confirming compatibility with a controlled EFT expansion organized around the finite-range hadronic scale. As a result, the observed $d^*(2380)$ pole emerges from the virtual state to bound state by using the EFT re-organization in this work.

</details>

### [26] [Dark Energy Stars in Finch-Skea Spacetime with a Schwarzschild-(Anti)-de Sitter Exterior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30398)
*Muhamad Ashraf Azman*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: This paper investigates how a cosmological constant $\\Lambda$ influences the structure and stability of dark energy stars modeled in Finch-Skea spacetime within Einstein gravity. Using the complexity factor formalism to derive the temporal metric component, the authors construct anisotropic stellar models composed of ordinary matter and dark energy, matched to Schwarzschild-(anti-)de Sitter exteriors. They find that positive $\\Lambda$ yields larger, less compact stars, while negative $\\Lambda$ produces denser, more compact configurations. Although energy conditions hold, large $|\\Lambda|$ disrupts hydrostatic equilibrium and compromises causal and dynamical stability.
\n\n
本文在爱因斯坦引力框架下系统研究了宇宙学常数 $\\Lambda$ 对Finch-Skea时空中的暗能量星结构与稳定性的影响。作者利用complexity factor formalism构建包含普通物质与暗能量的各向异性静态星体模型，并通过边界条件将其内解光滑匹配到Schwarzschild-(anti-)de Sitter外解。结果表明，正的 $\\Lambda$ 导致更大、更弥散的构型，而负的 $\\Lambda$ 则产生更致密、更紧致的星体。尽管所有情形均满足能量条件，但足够大的 $|\\Lambda|$ 会破坏流体静力学平衡，并影响模型的因果性与动力学稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from the need to understand how the cosmological constant—representing dark energy on cosmic scales—affects compact astrophysical objects like dark energy stars. While $\\Lambda$ is typically negligible locally, its cumulative or effective role in exotic stellar models warrants systematic study, especially in alternative or extended gravitational frameworks. The Finch-Skea spacetime provides a physically viable ansatz for stellar interiors, and incorporating $\\Lambda$ allows exploration of its impact on compactness, equilibrium, and stability under realistic boundary matching to asymptotically (A)dS exteriors.
\n\n
该研究的动机源于理解宇宙学常数（作为宇宙尺度上暗能量的代表）如何影响暗能量星等致密天体。尽管 $\\Lambda$ 在局域通常可忽略，但其在奇异星体模型中的累积或有效作用值得系统研究，尤其是在广义相对论或扩展引力框架下。Finch-Skea时空为恒星内部提供了物理上合理的度规假设，引入 $\\Lambda$ 可以探索其在匹配到渐近(A)dS外部时空条件下对致密性、平衡性及稳定性的影响。

Method: The authors adopt the Finch-Skea metric ansatz for the spatial component and use the complexity factor formalism—derived from the orthogonal splitting of the Riemann tensor—to determine the temporal metric potential, thereby closing the Einstein field equations for an anisotropic fluid. The matter content includes both ordinary baryonic matter and a dark energy component. The interior solution is matched at the stellar surface to the Schwarzschild-(anti-)de Sitter exterior via Israel-Darmois junction conditions, ensuring continuity of the first fundamental form. Structural properties (mass-radius relation, compactness), energy conditions, hydrostatic equilibrium (via Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff equation generalized with $\\Lambda$), and stability criteria (causality, adiabatic index) are then analyzed for varying $\\Lambda$ using the observed mass of Vela X-1 as input.
\n\n
作者采用Finch-Skea度规作为空间分量的假设，并利用complexity factor formalism（源自Riemann张量的正交分解）来确定时间度规势，从而封闭含各向异性流体的爱因斯坦场方程。物质内容包含普通重子物质和暗能量成分。内解通过Israel-Darmois连接条件在恒星表面与Schwarzschild-(anti-)de Sitter外解匹配，确保第一基本形式连续。随后，以Vela X-1的观测质量为输入，分析不同 $\\Lambda$ 下的结构特性（质量-半径关系、致密性）、能量条件、流体静力学平衡（通过含 $\\Lambda$ 的广义Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff方程）以及稳定性判据（因果性、绝热指数）。

Result: For the compact star candidate Vela X-1 ($M \\approx 1.77\\,M_\\odot$), positive cosmological constant ($\\Lambda > 0$) increases the stellar radius and decreases compactness $u = M/R$, while negative $\\Lambda$ decreases radius and enhances compactness and surface redshift. All energy conditions (null, weak, strong, dominant) are satisfied throughout the interior for moderate $|\\Lambda|$. However, large $|\\Lambda|$ introduces significant deviations from hydrostatic equilibrium, as quantified by the non-vanishing of the conservation equation's residual. Additionally, causality (sound speed $v_s^2 < 1$) and dynamical stability (adiabatic index $\\Gamma > 4/3$) are violated for sufficiently large $|\\Lambda|$, indicating a critical threshold beyond which the model becomes unphysical.
\n\n
对于致密星候选体Vela X-1（$M \\approx 1.77\\,M_\\odot$），正宇宙学常数（$\\Lambda > 0$）增大恒星半径并降低致密性 $u = M/R$，而负 $\\Lambda$ 则减小半径并增强致密性与表面红移。在中等大小的 $|\\Lambda|$ 下，所有能量条件（零、弱、强、主控）在恒星内部均被满足。然而，较大的 $|\\Lambda|$ 会导致显著偏离流体静力学平衡，表现为守恒方程残差非零。此外，当 $|\\Lambda|$ 足够大时，因果性（声速 $v_s^2 < 1$）和动力学稳定性（绝热指数 $\\Gamma > 4/3$）均被破坏，表明存在一个临界阈值，超过后模型不再物理合理。

Conclusion: The cosmological constant significantly modulates the global properties of dark energy stars in Finch-Skea spacetime, acting as an effective parameter that controls compactness, gravitational binding, and stability. While small $|\\Lambda|$ preserves physical viability, large values—whether positive or negative—destabilize the configuration by violating equilibrium and causality. However, sufficiently large values of the cosmological constant introduce deviations from hydrostatic equilibrium and affect the causal and dynamical stability behavior of the model. 作者考虑一个在有限维希尔伯特空间中幺正演化的量子态，其哈密顿量的能量本征值间隔是可通约的（即所有 $E_i - E_j$ 都是某一基频的有理数倍）。这保证了严格周期性：对某个周期 $T$，有 $|\psi(t+T)\rangle = |\psi(t)\rangle$。从最小熵初态出发，他们分析一个周期内的熵轨迹 $S(t)$，并证明由于量子干涉和能谱结构，熵可以表现出一个显著的激发，远超玻尔兹曼因子 $P(\Delta S) \propto \exp(-\Delta S)$ 的预期。该激发被识别为可观测的宇宙学历史。

Abstract: We put forward a quantum model of cosmology that is exactly periodic but avoids the Boltzmann Brain problem. If the universe is described by a quantum state evolving unitarily in a finite-dimensional Hilbert space, its evolution will be recurrent: given enough time, the state will return arbitrarily close to its initial state. Here we show that this is not necessarily true. If the differences in energy eigenvalues are commensurable, the evolution is not simple recurrent, but exactly periodic. Moreover, if the state starts at minimum thermodynamic entropy, its evolution can feature a distinguished entropy excursion that is much more pronounced than one would expect from the conventional expression $P(ΔS) \propto \exp(-ΔS)$. Abstract: A long-standing problem in the simulation of neutron star spacetimes is the treatment of vacuum regions outside the stars.
