The pseudo-vacuum treatment shows slightly larger but approximately constant violations and can improve the computation of fast tail ejecta as well as provide convergent gravitational waves of quality comparable to the standard atmosphere. Overall, results from different atmosphere treatments and a two-code comparison suggest that current computations of gravitational waves and (dynamical) ejecta in the presence of an artifical atmosphere are robust, provided that conservative adaptive mesh refinement with flux correction is employed.

Method: The authors employ the covariant Shiromizu-Maeda-Sasaki (SMS) formulation of the Randall-Sundrum II braneworld, which projects five-dimensional Einstein equations onto the 3+1-dimensional brane, yielding effective four-dimensional field equations with quadratic energy-momentum corrections and a projected Weyl tensor term. They combine the Gauss-Codazzi equations with local energy-momentum conservation on the brane to derive a consistent framework for fluid dynamics. The analysis focuses on the early radiation-dominated era where bulk effects are strongest.

Abstract: Higher-dimensional black holes have been extensively studied over the years, primarily from heuristic and fundamental perspectives or within the context of holographic applications. However, their interaction with ordinary matter confined to the brane is also of particular interest in cosmology. For certain regions of parameter space, PBHs that could contribute to the present-day dark matter abundance may have formed with masses below the effective four-dimensional Planck scale. The discrepancy between our black hole masses and the most optimistic previous estimates grows as $t^{0.34}$-a significant difference that reaches up to several orders of magnitude by the end of the strong-accretion epoch, particularly for black holes that form early and for small values of the fundamental Planck scale $M_5$, reaching up to $\sim 10^5$ for the smallest $M_5$ permitted by observations.

### [30] [Black hole evolutions: Lessons from bifurcation theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30458)
*Ivan Booth,Graham Cox,Chiamaka Mary Okpala*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: This paper investigates the non-unique time evolution of marginally outer trapped surfaces (MOTSs) when the associated stability operator becomes non-invertible, employing bifurcation theory to classify possible evolutionary behaviors—such as saddle-node, pitchfork, and transcritical bifurcations—and demonstrates these phenomena analytically and numerically in various spacetimes including Reissner-Nordström-de Sitter and Weyl-distorted Schwarzschild. The framework is generalizable to any gravitational theory admitting MOTS-like structures.
\n\n
本文研究了当关联的稳定性算子不可逆时，边缘外陷获面（marginally outer trapped surfaces, MOTSs）时间演化的非唯一性，采用分岔理论对可能的演化行为（如鞍结分岔、叉式分岔和跨临界分岔）进行分类，并在Reissner-Nordström-de Sitter和Weyl扭曲Schwarzschild等多种时空背景下通过解析与数值方法展示了这些现象。该框架可推广至任何包含MOTS或其推广结构的引力理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The uniqueness of MOTS evolution was previously established under the condition that the stability operator is invertible. However, at moments when this operator fails to be invertible, the evolution may become non-unique. The paper aims to systematically understand and classify such non-unique evolutions using tools from bifurcation theory, treating MOTSs as fixed points of a dynamical system defined by their defining equations.
\n\n
先前已有研究表明：当稳定性算子可逆时，MOTS的演化是唯一的。然而，在该算子不可逆的时刻，演化可能变得非唯一。本文旨在利用分岔理论系统地理解并分类此类非唯一演化，将MOTS视为由其定义方程所构成动力系统的不动点。

Method: The authors apply bifurcation theory, interpreting the MOTS-defining equation as a nonlinear system whose linearization around a solution yields the stability operator. When this operator has a zero eigenvalue (i.e., is non-invertible), standard implicit function theorem arguments fail, and bifurcation analysis becomes appropriate. They define a generalized stability operator and use both analytical calculations and numerical simulations to locate and classify bifurcations (saddle-node, pitchfork, transcritical) in spherically symmetric (Reissner-Nordström-de Sitter) and axisymmetric (Reissner-Nordström, Weyl-distorted Schwarzschild) spacetimes. They also examine partial barrier properties of unstable MOTSs.
\n\n
作者应用分岔理论，将MOTS定义方程视为一个非线性系统，其在解附近的线性化即为稳定性算子。当该算子具有零本征值（即不可逆）时，隐函数定理失效，此时需采用分岔分析。他们定义了一个广义稳定性算子，并结合解析计算与数值模拟，在球对称（Reissner-Nordström-de Sitter）和轴对称（Reissner-Nordström、Weyl扭曲Schwarzschild）时空中定位并分类分岔（鞍结、叉式、跨临界）。此外，他们还研究了不稳定MOTS的部分屏障性质。

Result: The paper identifies explicit examples of different types of bifurcations in the evolution of MOTSs: saddle-node bifurcations corresponding to pair creation/annihilation, as well as pitchfork and transcritical bifurcations, in specific exact and distorted black hole spacetimes. Conclusion: The discrete one-bit absorption framework provides a consistent derivation of logarithmic entropy corrections in higher-dimensional and charged black holes, with coefficients tied to physical absorption dynamics. Abstract: We reconsider the classical one bit absorption model of black hole growth as a discrete recursion rather than a continuum equation. Adding a mass or energy label reduces the endpoint degeneracy, but it does not remove it in the ensembles considered here. The corrected interpretation separates dynamical entropy, thermodynamic state entropy, history entropy, and hidden conditional entropy. It also clarifies that the multinomial formula is a restricted history counting result, not a proof that different histories correspond to unique or identical microscopic black hole states.

This framework accommodates measure-valued stress-energy tensors and distributional curvature (Weyl as Dirac masses, Ricci merely $L^1$).

作者采用 areal 规范，其中 $T^2$ 轨道的面积函数作为时间坐标。他们引入适配于此规范的新几何变量（如归一化联络系数）和流体变量，将 Einstein–Euler 系统重写为一阶拟线性平衡律系统：
$$
\partial_t U + \partial_x F(U) = S(U),
$$
并附带椭圆约束和熵不等式。所利用的关键结构特性包括：(i) 主部的双曲性，(ii) 非线性项中的 null forms（改善正则性），(iii) 熵流通过最大值原理给出 $L^1$–$L^\infty$ 估计，(iv) div-curl 结构支持补偿紧性方法，(v) 对联络系数的时空 $L^2$ 估计。“温和 Einstein–Euler 流”（tame Einstein–Euler flow）的解概念要求主变量属于 $L^2$（有限能量），次变量属于 $BV$（有界变差）。该框架可容纳测度值应力-能量张量和分布意义下的曲率（Weyl 曲率为 Dirac 质量，Ricci 曲率仅为 $L^1$）。

Result: The paper proves three main results: (1) Global existence of areal foliations for all $T^2$-symmetric initial data with finite energy (square-integrable connection coefficients) and bounded variation fluid variables, covering vacuum, scalar field, and general compressible fluid matter. (2) Nonlinear stability for “well-prepared” initial data near homogeneous states—solutions remain close and converge asymptotically. (3) Nonlinear instability for geometrically oscillatory initial data, where high-frequency oscillations generate measure-theoretic corrections to the stress-energy tensor (via Young measures or H-measures), leading to effective spacetime geometries differing from naive limits. Additionally, in the future-contracting regime ($t \searrow 0$), the areal time reaches zero generically (e.g., in non-vacuum Gowdy and vacuum toroidal symmetry), indicating a crushing singularity; in the expanding regime ($t \to \infty$), the foliation is complete.
\n\n
本文证明了三个主要结果：(1) 对所有具有有限能量（联络系数平方可积）和有界变差流体变量的 $T^2$ 对称初值，areal 叶状结构全局存在，涵盖真空、标量场和广义可压缩流体物质模型。(2) 对“良准备”的近齐次初值具有非线性稳定性——解保持接近并渐近收敛。(3) 对几何振荡初值具有非线性不稳定性，其中高频振荡通过 Young 测度或 H-测度在应力-能量张量中产生测度论修正项，导致有效时空几何偏离朴素极限。此外，在未来收缩区域（$t \searrow 0$），areal 时间普遍趋于零（例如在非真空 Gowdy 对称和真空环面对称情形），表明存在压碎型奇点；在未来膨胀区域（$t \to \infty$），叶状结构是完备的。

Conclusion: The work demonstrates that a robust global theory for Einstein-matter systems is possible even under very low regularity—specifically, with only square-integrable connection coefficients—by leveraging the intrinsic geometric and analytic structures of the equations (hyperbolicity, null forms, entropy, div-curl). This extends the scope of relativistic fluid dynamics and gravitational collapse beyond classical smooth settings, providing a mathematically rigorous framework for spacetimes containing both shock waves and impulsive gravitational waves. The results also shed light on the nature of cosmological singularities in symmetry-reduced models, supporting the idea that generic $T^2$-symmetric spacetimes end in a curvature singularity in the contracting direction, while expanding ones are geodesically complete.
\n\n
本工作表明，即使在极低正则性下（特别是联络系数仅需平方可积），通过利用方程内在的几何与分析结构（双曲性、null forms、熵结构、div-curl 结构），仍可建立爱因斯坦-物质系统的稳健全局理论。这将相对论流体动力学与引力坍缩的研究范围扩展到经典光滑情形之外，为包含激波与脉冲引力波的时空提供了严格的数学框架。结果也揭示了对称性约化模型中宇宙学奇点的本质，支持如下观点：一般 $T^2$ 对称时空在未来收缩方向终结于曲率奇点，而在膨胀方向则是测地完备的。

Abstract: We study the global evolution problem for the Einstein equations under T2 symmetry on T3, allowing vacuum, scalar-field, and compressible-fluid matter models, governed by a general equation of state including isothermal and polytropic fluids. Under this symmetry, we obtain the first non-perturbative, global existence and stability theory with connection coefficients being merely square-integrable, which allows both impulsive gravitational waves and shock waves. In areal gauge, we introduce new fluid and geometric variables and reformulate the Einstein-Euler system as a first-order system of nonlinear balance laws with constraints and an entropy structure. The resulting formulation exhibits hyperbolicity, null forms, entropy currents, div-curl structure, maximum principles, and spacetime estimates. This leads to a notion of tame Einstein-Euler flow for which the essential geometric and fluid variables are square-integrable (finite energy), and the secondary variables are absolutely continuous (or, more generally, of bounded variation). In this non-perturbative and weak regularity setting, the equations remain meaningful even when the Weyl curvature concentrates into Dirac masses along timelike hypersurfaces, and the Ricci curvature remains only integrable. Our main results are a global existence theorem for areal foliations, a nonlinear stability theorem for well-prepared initial data, and a nonlinear instability theorem for geometrically oscillatory data, the latter producing measure corrections to the stress energy tensor. In the future-contracting regime, the areal foliation reaches a geometric singularity where the volume of T3 spatial slices degenerates to zero. The areal function reaches zero generically in the non-vacuum Gowdy-symmetric and vacuum torus-symmetric cases. In the future-expanding regime, the areal foliation is complete.

TL;DR: This paper introduces a fully simulation-based method to generate strong gravitational lensing images by self-consistently extracting lenses, sources, and line-of-sight (LoS) structures from cosmological simulations. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current strong-lensing forecasts rely on hybrid methods that combine simulated lenses with analytic or observed source populations near caustics, often neglecting or improperly modeling correlated and uncorrelated line-of-sight (LoS) structure. This stems from a geometric mismatch between the pencil-beam nature of lensing observations and cubic simulation boxes, as well as the trade-off between simulation volume and resolution. The authors aim to overcome these limitations by developing a fully self-consistent, simulation-based framework that captures the full 3D large-scale structure along the line of sight.

当前的强透镜预测依赖于混合方法，将模拟透镜与解析或观测的源星系（位于焦散线附近）相结合，通常忽略或不恰当地处理关联与非关联的视线方向（LoS）结构。这一问题源于透镜观测的铅笔光束几何特性与立方模拟盒子之间的不匹配，以及模拟体积与分辨率之间的权衡。作者旨在通过构建一个完全自洽的、基于模拟的框架来克服这些限制，该框架能够捕捉沿视线方向完整的三维大尺度结构。

Method: The authors develop a structure-preserving remapping technique to transform standard periodic cubic simulation volumes into lensing-appropriate light-cone geometries. They then perform multi-plane ray tracing through the resulting particle data, treating all resolved halos—including the main cluster lens, background sources, and intervening LoS structures—as discrete deflection planes. This allows them to generate strong-lensing images directly from simulations while maintaining physical correlations across redshift. The method is implemented using the IllustrisTNG simulation suite.

作者开发了一种结构保持的重映射技术，将标准周期性立方模拟体积转换为适合透镜研究的光锥几何结构。随后，他们对所得粒子数据进行多平面光线追踪，将所有已分辨的晕（包括主团簇透镜、背景源和中间LoS结构）视为离散的偏转平面。这使得他们能够直接从模拟中生成强透镜图像，同时保持跨红移的物理关联性。该方法在IllustrisTNG模拟套件上实现。

Result: Using IllustrisTNG, the authors generate strong-lensing light cones and images. They find that uncorrelated LoS structure can shift lensed image positions by several arcseconds, induces a $\\sim 6\\%$ scatter in the area of the primary critical curve, and alters the total critical area within 100$^{\\prime\\prime}$ of the cluster potential minimum by $16^{+20\\%}_{-14\\%}$ at source redshift $z_s = 4$. Conclusion: A fully simulation-based strong-lensing pipeline that self-consistently includes all resolved line-of-sight structure is both feasible and necessary for precision cosmology. Standard hybrid approaches that neglect or approximate LoS effects may introduce systematic biases in inferred cluster masses and cosmological parameters. \n\n
一个完全基于模拟的强透镜流程——自洽地包含所有已分辨的视线方向（LoS）结构——对于精确宇宙学而言既是可行的，也是必要的。忽略或近似处理LoS效应的标准混合方法可能在推断团簇质量及宇宙学参数时引入系统性偏差。所提出的方法为生成逼真的透镜模拟样本和评估当前及未来巡天中LoS引起的不确定性提供了稳健框架。

Abstract: Galaxy cluster strong gravitational lensing plays a central role in precision cosmology, yet robust theoretical predictions have lagged behind an abundance of high-quality strong lensing observations. This shortfall reflects both a mismatch between the geometry of the strong-lensing problem and standard cubic simulation boxes, and the fundamental tension between simulation volume and resolution. Consequently, many current forecasts adopt hybrid approaches that extract individual lenses from simulations and combine them with analytic or observed source populations positioned near caustics. These methods often omit correlated and/or uncorrelated line-of-sight (LoS) structure, or include it in ways that do not preserve correlations across redshift. Here we present a fully simulation-based procedure that generates strong-lensing images directly from particle data, drawing the lens, source, and all intervening resolved objects self-consistently from the simulated large-scale structure. Our approach combines a structure-preserving remapping of the simulation volume into a lensing-appropriate geometry with multi-plane ray tracing, enabling the use of uniform simulation boxes that resolve both cluster-scale primary lenses and high-redshift source galaxies. We find that uncorrelated LoS structure can shift the relative positions of lensed images by several arcseconds, introduces a $\sim 6\%$ scatter in the area of a cluster's primary critical curve, and changes the total critical area within 100$^{\prime\prime}$ of the cluster potential minimum by $16^{+20\%}_{-14\%}$ at a source plane redshift of $z_s=4$.

Abstract: Measurements of tangential drifts of distant quasars and galactic nuclei on the celestial sphere provide a novel and independent method of testing cosmological hypotheses. In this work, we employ an axisymmetric Bianchi I model as a relatively simple phenomenological model that is useful for quantifying departures from the cosmological principle. Using a quality-filtered sample of 1.2 million proper motion vectors of distant quasars from Gaia Data Release 3, we perform global fits of the position drift fields with vector spherical harmonics (VSH) to second degree for five non-overlapping subsets of the sources with redshifts from 0.5 to 3, and assess the ability of the Bianchi I model to describe the signal. Although a significant quadrupole signal is detected in each bin, the increase of the amplitude of the signal with redshift predicted by the Bianchi I model is not confidently confirmed.   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Galaxy clusters exhibit significant scatter in scaling relations and other properties, which is likely tied to their diverse late-time assembly histories. Method: Using the IllustrisTNG cosmological magnetohydrodynamical simulations, the authors define subsamples of galaxy clusters selected by extreme values of various structural parameters—both intrinsic (e.g., center-of-mass offset, virial ratio) and observable proxies (e.g., magnitude gap $\\Delta m_{12}$, stellar mass asymmetry). For each selection criterion, they compute the median assembly history (e.g., mass accretion rate or formation redshift) of the selected subsamples and quantify the correlation strength. The analysis includes both 3D intrinsic measures and 2D projected versions mimicking observational conditions.

作者利用IllustrisTNG宇宙学磁流体动力学模拟，根据多种结构参数的极值（包括本征量如质心偏移、位力比，以及可观测代理量如星等间隙 $\\Delta m_{12}$ 和恒星质量不对称性）定义星系团子样本。针对每种选择标准，计算所选子样本的中位数组装历史（如质量吸积率或形成红移），并量化相关性强度。分析同时包含三维本征量和模拟观测条件的二维投影版本。

Result: Several structural parameters—particularly the magnitude gap between the first and second brightest cluster galaxies ($\\Delta m_{12}$) and the projected asymmetry of the stellar mass distribution—show strong correlations with recent mass accretion. 多个结构参数——尤其是第一与第二亮星系之间的星等间隙（$\\Delta m_{12}$）和恒星质量分布的投影不对称性——与近期质量吸积表现出强相关性。通过这些标准选出的“弛豫”星系团具有系统性更早的组装历史，而“非弛豫”样本则对应更近期的重大并合或吸积事件。即使仅使用投影的、可观测的量，这些相关性依然显著。

Conclusion: Structural parameters, especially those based on stellar light or mass distribution (e.g., magnitude gap, asymmetry), serve as robust observational proxies for galaxy cluster assembly history. The strong link between present-day structure and formation history implies that structural classification should be explicitly accounted for in studies of scaling relations, selection functions, and average density profiles to avoid biases from uncontrolled assembly-history variation.

基于恒星光度或质量分布的结构参数（如星等间隙、不对称性）可作为星系团组装历史的稳健观测代理。当前结构与形成历史之间的强关联表明，在标度关系、选择函数（selection functions）和平均密度轮廓的研究中，应明确纳入结构分类，以避免因未控制的组装历史差异引入系统偏差。

Abstract: As the largest and most recently formed stage of hierarchical structure, present-day galaxy clusters are predicted to have a broad range of late-time assembly histories. This diversity may explain much of the scatter in scaling relations and other cluster properties. Observationally, systems with more or less recent accretion should appear as unrelaxed and relaxed clusters, respectively. We find that several observable quantities, including the magnitude gap between the brightest galaxies and the asymmetry of the stellar mass distribution, are very effective in selecting cluster samples with more or less recent accretion, even when applied in projection. Given the strong correlations between assembly history and present-day cluster structure, we suggest that structural classification be included explicitly in any analysis of catalogue completeness, scaling relations, or mean density profiles.

本文提出利用星系团中强引力透镜的残差功率谱 $ \mathrm{P}_\delta(k) $ 作为统计探针，以区分波状暗物质（$ \psi $DM）与冷暗物质（CDM）。通过模拟JWST质量的观测数据，作者表明 $ \mathrm{P}_\delta(k) $ 对 $ \psi $DM 的超轻玻色子质量（$ m \sim 10^{-22} $ eV）及其涨落幅度敏感，并可在尺度 $ 1 \lesssim k \lesssim 11\,\mathrm{kpc}^{-1} $ 上统计显著地区分 $ \psi $DM 与 CDM 子晕。

Upcoming LSS surveys will probe small scales with high precision, offering new sensitivity to such features. However, extracting this information requires modeling their non-linear evolution into the matter power spectrum, which is not captured by linear perturbation theory. This motivates the need for accurate non-linear predictions tailored to wiggly PPS scenarios.

尽管最简慢滚暴胀模型预言了一个平滑、近标度不变的原初功率谱（PPS），且与当前宇宙微波背景（CMB）数据一致，但许多有理论动机的非最小暴胀模型（如场空间中存在尖锐特征、台阶或转向）会在 PPS 中产生振荡型偏离——即“振荡”。未来的大型结构（LSS）巡天将在小尺度上提供高精度测量，对这类特征具有新的灵敏度。然而，要提取这些信息，必须建模这些特征在物质功率谱中的非线性演化，而线性微扰理论无法描述这一过程。这促使人们需要针对“振荡型 PPS”场景发展精确的非线性预测方法。

Abstract: The simplest models of slow-roll inflation predict a featureless, nearly scale-invariant power spectrum of primordial curvature perturbations, consistent with current observations. However, in many non-minimal realisations of inflation, one generically expects the primordial power spectrum (PPS) to be ''wiggly'' with features that strongly deviate from scale-invariance. Current and next generation large scale structure (LSS) surveys will probe the PPS with unprecedented accuracy and therefore also increase sensitivity to power spectrum wiggles. However, accessing the information contained in these data will require an understanding of the behaviour of wiggly power spectra beyond the linear regime of structure formation. In this work, we use high resolution $N$-body simulations to study the non-linear evolution of scenarios in which the PPS has superimposed oscillations, calibrate a one-parameter semi-analytic damping model to describe their signatures in the late-time matter power spectrum and test the relative improvement on constraints by implementing this modelling strategy in a likelihood analysis via Gaussian Process Regression (GPR) emulation. Paying special attention to identifying our approach's domain of validity and quantifying as well as propagating uncertainties, we demonstrate that as long as the frequency of the PPS modulation is large enough, we are able to predict the matter power spectrum with sub-percent accuracy -- thereby enabling us to search for inflationary wiggles in LSS data.

*Chiara Animali,Baptiste Blachier,Nanoka Okada,Christophe Ringeval,Tomo Takahashi,Koki Tokeshi*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Standard stochastic inflation evolves the inflaton field forward in time under quantum diffusion, but this obscures the perspective of observers who necessarily reside on the end-of-inflation hypersurface. Time-reversed stochastic inflation addresses this by evolving backward from the end of inflation, enforcing a physically motivated boundary condition tied to observable reality. The authors aim to explore how such a reformulation alters statistical predictions, especially in bounded potentials where global structure matters.

标准随机暴胀在量子扩散下将暴胀子场向前演化，但这模糊了必然位于暴胀结束超曲面上的观测者的视角。时间反演随机暴胀通过从暴胀结束时刻向后演化来解决这一问题，施加了与可观测现实相关的物理边界条件。作者旨在探索这种重构如何改变统计预言，特别是在全局结构起关键作用的有界势中。

Method: The authors formulate and exactly solve the time-reversed Fokker–Planck equation for stochastic inflation in a flat, bounded potential—the quantum well—defined between two absorbing boundaries. They compute the probability distribution of the number of e-folds $N$ accumulated from an initial field value to the end of inflation, and derive the corresponding distribution of curvature perturbations $\\zeta$, leveraging the relation $\\zeta \\approx -N$. The analysis distinguishes between short- and long-lifetime regimes, identifying when stochastic effects dominate and erase initial conditions.
\n\n
作者构建并精确求解了在平坦有界势（即量子阱，由两个吸收边界界定）中随机暴胀的时间反演Fokker–Planck方程。他们计算了从初始场值到暴胀结束所累积的e-fold数$N$的概率分布，并利用关系式$\\zeta \\approx -N$推导出相应的曲率扰动$\\zeta$分布。分析区分了短寿命和长寿命机制，识别出随机效应何时主导并抹除初始条件的记忆。

Result: Two distinct dynamical regimes emerge: (i) for short lifetimes, the field statistics match those of a semi-infinite flat potential; (ii) for long lifetimes, enhanced stochasticity erases memory of the initial state. The curvature perturbation distribution $P(\\zeta)$ reproduces the standard result for small $|\\zeta|$, but develops symmetric exponential tails $P(\\zeta) \\sim e^{-2|\\zeta|/\\sigma^2}$ for large fluctuations—twice as steep as the $e^{-|\\zeta|/\\sigma^2}$ tail in forward stochastic inflation.
\n\n
研究揭示了两种不同的动力学机制：(i) 在短寿命情况下，场的统计特性与半无限平坦势的结果一致；(ii) 在长寿命情况下，增强的随机性抹除了初始状态的记忆。曲率扰动分布$P(\\zeta)$在小$|\\zeta|$时复现标准结果，但在大起伏时发展出对称的指数尾部$P(\\zeta) \\sim e^{-2|\\zeta|/\\sigma^2}$——其衰减速率是前向随机暴胀中$e^{-|\\zeta|/\\sigma^2}$尾部的两倍。

Conclusion: Time-reversed stochastic inflation provides a physically consistent framework aligned with observer-centric boundary conditions at the end of inflation. Abstract: Time-reversed stochastic inflation solves the stochastic evolution of the inflationary universe backward in time, by counting the number of e-folds from the end of quantum diffusion towards some initial state. The point of view of observers attached to the end-of-inflation hypersurface is thus enforced. In this work, we exactly solve time-reversed stochastic inflation in a flat and bounded potential, the so-called quantum well. Such tails arise for both positive and negative values, and decay twice as fast as the one obtained in the standard forward stochastic inflation. These differences may have important consequences for tail-sensitive phenomena, such as primordial black hole formation.

Abstract: We present a novel possibility that a network of domain walls bounded by cosmic strings generates a stochastic gravitational wave background (SWGB) signal originating from the spontaneous breaking of a gauged $U(1)_F$ flavor symmetry and the subsequent breaking of discrete $Z_2$ symmetry that accommodates dark matter. This is then used for rapid inference in the detector-specific signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) computation which also makes the process fast and efficient.   <summary>Details</summary>

Method: The authors employ a Pauli reduction of the planar Dirac equation under a spatially uniform $\utwo$ Yang–Mills field derived from the Dossa–Avossevou construction. This yields an effective Hamiltonian acting on $\mathbb{C}^{2}_{\mathrm{spin}}\otimes\mathbb{C}^{2}_{\mathrm{iso}}$, featuring a constant operator composed of a diagonal term $σ^{3}T^{3}$ and off-diagonal terms $σ^{1}T^{1}+σ^{2}T^{2}$. Diagonalization of this operator produces three eigenvalues ($λ_{\mathrm{FM}}, λ_S, λ_T$), with degeneracies and scaling behaviors analyzed in terms of gauge coupling $g$, non-Abelian amplitude $β$, and scalar amplitude $ρ$.

Result: Diagonalization yields one doubly degenerate ferromagnetic branch with eigenvalue $λ_{\mathrm{FM}} = \frac{g^{2}β^{2}}{4m}$ and two non-degenerate mixed branches $λ_S = -\frac{g^{2}β(β - 2ρ)}{4m}$ and $λ_T = -\frac{g^{2}β(β + 2ρ)}{4m}$. Abstract: We investigate a planar Dirac oscillator coupled to a spatially uniform \(\utwo=\uone\times\su\) Yang--Mills background.   <summary>Details</summary>

对于几乎所有轴子质量 $m_a$ 和耦合常数 $g$ 的取值，带有无穷小电荷的旋转极端黑洞都会发展出奇异性视界。在大 $m_a$ 极限下，这些奇异性重现了爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦理论中高阶导数修正所导致的奇异性。在略微偏离极端性时，诱导出的潮汐力异常增大，表明低能有效描述在此失效。

Conclusion: Axion-induced singularities reveal a generic pathology in the classical description of near-extremal black holes, suggesting that consistent UV completions (e.g., string theory) must resolve or avoid these singular structures. Abstract: We show that axions cause extremal black holes to have singular horizons. When the axion mass becomes large, these singularities are related to the recently discovered singularities induced by higher-derivative corrections to the Einstein-Maxwell equations. Away from extremality, this effect produces anomalously large tidal forces in the vicinity of near-extremal horizons, causing breakdown of the effective theory.

Abstract: We construct matrices that shift the scaling dimension of scalar fields for arbitrary de Sitter Feynman diagrams. Abstract: We develop a geometric formulation of vertex operators in superstring theory based on integral forms on super Riemann surfaces. Abstract: We revisit the shifted-tree decomposition formula proposed in our previous work arXiv:2410.17192 for two-site cosmological wavefunction coefficients. Abstract: Chord diagrams encode boundary correlators in the double-scaled holographic Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev model, but currently capture only a limited class of bulk interactions that yield pure power-law correlators. Key tools include variation of the action, soliton classification, and symmetry analysis via Killing vectors.
\n\n
作者从包含度规、dilaton和$n$-form场强$H_{(n)}$的有效D-brane作用量出发，导出一个推广Ricci流的耦合几何流系统。他们借鉴Perelman的方法，定义一个场依赖的体积元，并构造一个修正的熵型泛函，在该体积固定时证明其单调性。对于稳态孤子，他们证明流方程退化为gradient soliton条件。随后将分析推广至含Chern-Simons项的作用量，此时需引入一个新的Perelman型泛函，其中包含Chern-Simons贡献以保证一致性和单调性。关键工具包括作用量变分、孤子分类以及通过Killing矢量进行的对称性分析。

Result: The paper establishes: (1) a generalized geometric flow incorporating arbitrary $n$-form field strengths derived from D-brane actions; (2) monotonicity of the flow under fixed field-dependent volume, via a Perelman-type functional; (3) proof that all steady solitons are gradient solitons, implying the existence of a Killing vector field preserving all background fields in certain cases; (4) explicit identification of NS5 and D5 branes as gradient solitons with T-duality or S-duality invariant volumes; (5) extension to systems with Chern-Simons terms, yielding a new functional with a Chern-Simons piece that preserves monotonicity and the gradient soliton property under suitable assumptions.
\n\n
本文取得以下成果：(1) 建立了一个源自D-brane作用量、包含任意$n$-form场强的广义几何流；(2) 通过Perelman型泛函，在固定场依赖体积条件下证明了流的单调性；(3) 证明所有稳态孤子均为gradient soliton，并在某些情形下推出存在保持所有背景场不变的Killing矢量场；(4) 明确识别NS5和D5 brane为gradient soliton，其对应体积分别为T-duality不变体积或其S-dual；(5) 将框架推广至含Chern-Simons项的系统，得到一个包含Chern-Simons项的新泛函，在适当假设下仍保持单调性及gradient soliton性质。

Conclusion: The work provides a unified geometric flow framework for string-theoretic backgrounds with form fields and branes, extending Perelman’s ideas beyond pure gravity. The identification of physical brane solutions as gradient solitons links geometric analysis to string duality symmetries. The inclusion of Chern-Simons terms—ubiquitous in supergravity—demonstrates the robustness of the approach. This paves the way for using geometric flow techniques to study stability, classification, and duality properties of non-perturbative string vacua.
\n\n
本工作为包含形式场和brane的弦论背景提供了一个统一的几何流框架，将Perelman的思想拓展至纯引力之外。将物理brane解识别为gradient soliton，建立了几何分析与弦对偶对称性之间的联系。对超引力中普遍存在的Chern-Simons项的纳入，展示了该方法的鲁棒性。这为利用几何流技术研究非微扰弦真空的稳定性、分类及对偶性质开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Based on effective D-brane actions, we present a generalisation of the Ricci flow that includes the flow of a theory with a $n$-form field strength for $n\geq 0$. This is a generalisation of both the Ricci flows and the generalised Ricci flows. Following Perelman, we show that flows that keep a suitable field-dependent volume fixed are monotonic. We also show that all steady brane flow solitons are gradient solitons and use this to demonstrate that on some occasions this implies the existence of a Killing vector field that leaves all the other fields invariant. Particular cases of gradient solitons are NS5 and D5 branes, and the volume which is kept fixed in these cases is the T-duality invariant volume (NS5 brane) or its S-dual (D5 brane). We also generalise the above analysis to gravitational actions coupled to form gauge potentials that also exhibit a Chern-Simons type term. We find an alteration is required in the adaptation of Perelman's modification to this case, which yields a new functional that also exhibits a Chern-Simons term. Under suitable assumptions, we proceed to prove the monotonicity of the flow and that all steady flow solitons are gradient solitons. We also explore the consequences of the last statement on the geometry of solitons.

</details>


### [55] [Higher-Derivative Corrections to Reissner--Nordström Black Holes from Worldline QFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31331)
*Siddarth Ajith,Ravisankar Rajagopal,Nur Rifat,Diana Vaman,Kent Yagi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: This paper computes corrections to the Reissner-Nordström black hole geometry induced by higher-derivative $RF^2$ operators in the Einstein-Maxwell effective action, using both worldline QFT techniques (at leading post-Minkowskian order) and exact solutions of the modified field equations (to all orders in $G$). It shows that the weak gravity conjecture ensures non-negative temperature for extremal black holes, thereby excluding Drummond-Hathrell theory.
\n\n
本文计算了爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦有效作用量中高阶导数 $RF^2$ 算符对Reissner-Nordström黑洞几何的修正，采用了worldline QFT方法（在leading post-Minkowskian阶）和修正场方程的精确解（对牛顿常数 $G$ 全阶展开）。结果表明，弱引力猜想（weak gravity conjecture）保证了极端黑洞温度非负，从而排除了Drummond-Hathrell理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Higher-derivative corrections to classical black hole solutions are essential for understanding quantum gravity effects within the framework of Effective Field Theory (EFT). Specifically, $RF^2$ terms—quadratic in the Maxwell field strength $F_{\mu\nu}$ and linear in the Riemann tensor $R_{\mu\nu\rho\sigma}$—naturally appear in low-energy EFTs coupled to gravity. The authors aim to systematically derive how these corrections deform the Reissner-Nordström solution, examine thermodynamic consistency via the first law of black hole mechanics, and explore implications for swampland constraints like the Weak Gravity Conjecture (WGC).
\n\n
在有效场论（Effective Field Theory, EFT）框架下，经典黑洞解的高阶导数修正是理解量子引力效应的关键。特别是 $RF^2$ 项——麦克斯韦场强 $F_{\mu\nu}$ 的二次项与黎曼张量 $R_{\mu\nu\rho\sigma}$ 的线性耦合——自然出现在低能引力耦合EFT中。作者旨在系统推导这些修正如何改变Reissner-Nordström解，通过黑洞力学第一定律检验热力学一致性，并探索其对Swampland约束（如弱引力猜想 Weak Gravity Conjecture, WGC）的影响。

Method: The authors employ two complementary approaches: (1) worldline quantum field theory (QFT) methods to compute the leading-order post-Minkowskian correction to the black hole metric sourced by $RF^2$ interactions; and (2) direct integration of the full nonlinear Einstein-Maxwell equations modified by the $RF^2$ Lagrangian terms, yielding an exact solution valid to all orders in Newton's constant $G$. They then apply standard Wald entropy formalism to compute black hole entropy and analyze the near-horizon geometry of extremal solutions to extract temperature corrections.
\n\n
作者采用两种互补方法：(1) 使用worldline量子场论（QFT）方法计算由 $RF^2$ 相互作用引起的黑洞度规的leading-order post-Minkowskian修正；(2) 直接求解由 $RF^2$ 拉氏量项修正后的完整非线性爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦方程，得到对牛顿常数 $G$ 全阶有效的精确解。随后，他们应用标准的Wald熵形式计算黑洞熵，并分析极端解的近视界几何以提取温度修正。

Result: The corrected Reissner-Nordström metric is obtained explicitly. The entropy receives corrections proportional to the coefficients of the $RF^2$ operators. Crucially, the temperature of the extremal black hole becomes negative unless the coefficient of the $RF^2$ term satisfies a positivity bound equivalent to the Weak Gravity Conjecture. This excludes the specific case of Drummond-Hathrell effective theory, which violates this bound and yields unphysical negative temperatures for extremal black holes.
\n\n
修正后的Reissner-Nordström度规被显式求得。黑洞熵获得与 $RF^2$ 算符系数成正比的修正。关键的是，除非 $RF^2$ 项的系数满足一个等价于弱引力猜想（Weak Gravity Conjecture）的正定性界限，否则极端黑洞的温度将变为负值。这排除了Drummond-Hathrell有效理论这一特例，因其违反该界限，导致极端黑洞出现非物理的负温度。

Conclusion: The study demonstrates that consistency of black hole thermodynamics—specifically the requirement of non-negative extremal temperature—imposes stringent constraints on allowed higher-derivative corrections in gravitational EFTs. These constraints align precisely with swampland criteria such as the Weak Gravity Conjecture, providing a concrete link between black hole physics and quantum gravity consistency conditions. The exclusion of Drummond-Hathrell theory underscores the physical relevance of these bounds.
\n\n
本研究表明，黑洞热力学的一致性——特别是极端黑洞温度非负的要求——对引力有效场论（gravitational EFTs）中允许的高阶导数修正施加了严格限制。这些限制与Swampland判据（如弱引力猜想 Weak Gravity Conjecture）精确一致，为黑洞物理与量子引力一致性条件之间建立了具体联系。Drummond-Hathrell理论被排除，凸显了这些界限的物理意义。

Abstract: In this paper we derived the corrections to the Reissner-Nordström black hole when higher-derivative $RF^2$ terms (contractions of the Riemann tensor with the Maxwell field strength squared) are added to the Einstein-Maxwell action. Such terms arise naturally in the context of effective field theories. We used wordline QFT methods to obtain the leading order post-Minkowskian corrections. We verified these results by solving the modified Einstein-Maxwell field equations in closed form, to all orders in Newton's constant $G$. We discussed the first law and computed the entropy of the perturbed black holes. The extremal black hole temperature is non-negative precisely when the weak gravity conjecture is satisfied. This condition on the extremal black hole temperature rules out Drummond-Hathrell theory.

</details>


### [56] [Two roles of Alexander in two Kashaev phases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31588)
*Dmitry Galakhov,Alexei Morozov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: This paper explores the ambiguity in non-perturbative resurgence theory through the Kashaev double-scaling limit of Chern-Simons Wilson loop averages, revealing two coexisting quasiclassical phases: one linked to classical $A$-polynomials and hyperbolic volumes, and another yielding inverse Alexander polynomials $Δ^{-1}$, with consistency ensured by the structure of quantum $A$-polynomials.
\n\n
本文通过Chern-Simons理论中Wilson圈平均值的Kashaev双尺度极限，探讨了非微扰resurgence理论中的模糊性，揭示了两个共存的准经典相：一个与经典$A$-多项式和双曲体积相关，另一个则给出Alexander多项式的倒数$Δ^{-1}$，其一致性由量子$A$-多项式的特殊结构保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The motivation stems from understanding the intrinsic ambiguity in non-perturbative completions within resurgence theory—manifested via contour choices or multiple solutions to Ward identities—and using exactly solvable models like Chern-Simons theory to clarify how perturbative and non-perturbative sectors consistently unify. The Kashaev limit offers a controlled setting where quasiclassical methods reveal unexpected dual roles of topological invariants.
\n\n
动机源于理解resurgence理论中非微扰补全所固有的模糊性——表现为积分围道的选择或Ward恒等式的多重解——并利用Chern-Simons理论等可精确求解模型来阐明微扰与非微扰部分如何一致统一。Kashaev极限提供了一个受控环境，其中准经典方法揭示了拓扑不变量出人意料的双重角色。

Method: The authors analyze Wilson loop expectation values in Chern-Simons theory under the Kashaev double-scaling limit (large representation $R$, small coupling $k \to \infty$ with $R/k$ fixed), interpreted via quasiclassical/WKB approximation. They examine the asymptotic behavior of Jones polynomials and their relation to Alexander polynomials $Δ$, while studying the quantum $A$-polynomial that governs recursion relations. The key is identifying two distinct saddle-point branches in the path integral: one with non-vanishing classical action and another with vanishing action.
\n\n
作者在Kashaev双尺度极限（大表示$R$、小耦合$k \to \infty$且保持$R/k$固定）下分析Chern-Simons理论中的Wilson圈期望值，并通过准经典/WKB近似进行诠释。他们研究Jones多项式的渐近行为及其与Alexander多项式$Δ$的关系，同时考察支配递推关系的量子$A$-多项式。关键是识别路径积分中两个不同的鞍点分支：一个具有非零经典作用量，另一个则具有零作用量。

Result: In the Kashaev limit, Jones polynomials asymptotically approach $Δ^{-1}$, while classical $A$-polynomials share common roots with $Δ$. These seemingly contradictory behaviors are reconciled by the quantum $A$-polynomial, which admits two distinct quasiclassical solutions: a “geometric” branch yielding hyperbolic volumes and classical $A$-polynomials, and a “topological” branch producing $Δ^{-1}$. This demonstrates phase coexistence in the semiclassical limit of Chern-Simons theory.
\n\n
在Kashaev极限下，Jones多项式渐近趋于$Δ^{-1}$，而经典$A$-多项式与$Δ$具有公共根。这些看似矛盾的行为通过量子$A$-多项式得以调和：它允许两个不同的准经典解——一个“几何”分支给出双曲体积和经典$A$-多项式，另一个“拓扑”分支产生$Δ^{-1}$。这表明Chern-Simons理论的半经典极限中存在相共存。

Conclusion: The apparent paradox between the roles of Alexander polynomials—as divisors of classical $A$-polynomials and as inverses of perturbative Jones polynomials—is resolved by recognizing two coexisting quasiclassical phases in Chern-Simons theory. This underscores the necessity of considering multiple non-perturbative saddles in resurgence frameworks and provides a concrete mechanism for ambiguity cancellation, with implications for general Yang-Mills theories.
\n\n
Alexander多项式看似矛盾的角色——既是经典$A$-多项式的因子，又是微扰Jones多项式的倒数——通过认识到Chern-Simons理论中两个共存的准经典相得以解决。这强调了在resurgence框架中必须考虑多个非微扰鞍点，并为模糊性抵消提供了具体机制，对一般Yang-Mills理论具有启示意义。

Abstract: The crucial feature of resurgence theory is the ambiguity of non-perturbative behavior, reflected either in the different choices of integration contours or in the existence of several solutions to Ward identities. This is well illustrated by considering exactly solvable models, of which the prominent example is Chern-Simons theory. Its important chapter, which should have a direct generalization to arbitrary Yang-Mills, is the consideration of Wilson averages in the double-scaling limit of large representation and small coupling. For historical reasons, we call it a Kashaev limit. It possesses a natural interpretation in terms of quasiclassical/WKB approximation, which is, however, somewhat peculiar and thus sheds new light on the old story. The crucial point is the appearance of Alexander polynomials $Δ$ in two seemingly opposite roles: the classical $A$-polynomials have common roots with $Δ$, while Jones polynomials tend to $Δ^{-1}$ in the perturbative expansion. The consistency is provided by the peculiar form of the quantum $A$-polynomial, and the resolution of the puzzle is the co-existence of two different branches (phases) in the quasiclassical limit -- with non-trivial and with vanishing classical actions. The first leads to classical $A$-polynomials and hyperbolic volumes, the second -- to inverse Alexanders.

</details>
